Resistance\n        x = branch_data[k, 3]  # Reactance\n        b_ch = branch_data[k, 4]  # Total line charging susceptance\n        tap = branch_data[k, 8]  # Transformer tap ratio\n        shift = branch_data[k, 9]  # Transformer phase shift (degrees)\n        rate = branch_data[k, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Branch series admittance y = g + j*b\n        z_mag_sq = r**2 + x**2\n        if z_mag_sq > 1e-10:\n            g = r / z_mag_sq\n            b = -x / z_mag_sq\n        else:\n            g = 0\n            b = 0\n\n        # Transformer complex ratio T = tap * e^(j*shift)\n        # T = tap * (cos(shift) + j*sin(shift))\n        shift_rad = shift * np.pi / 180\n        T_real = tap * np.cos(shift_rad)\n        T_imag = tap * np.sin(shift_rad)\n        T_mag_sq = tap**2\n\n        # Shunt charging admittances\n        b_sh_i = b_ch / 2\n        b_sh_j = b_ch / 2\n\n        # Add to admittance matrix\n        # From-side self admittance: (y + j*b_sh_i) / |T|^2\n        G[i, i] += g / T_mag_sq\n        B[i, i] += (b + b_sh_i) / T_mag_sq\n\n        # To-side self admittance: y + j*b_sh_j\n        G[j, j] += g\n        B[j, j] += b + b_sh_j\n\n        # Mutual admittances: -y / T*  and  -y / T\n        # -y / T* = -(g + j*b) / (T_real - j*T_imag)\n        #         = -(g + j*b) * (T_real + j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n        #         = (-(g*T_real - b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real - g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n        G[i, j] += -(g*T_real - b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        B[i, j] += -(b*T_real + g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        # -y / T = -(g + j*b) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n        #        = -(g + j*b) * (T_real - j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n        #        = (-(g*T_real + b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real + g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n        G[j, i] += -(g*T_real + b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        B[j, i] += -(b*T_real - g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        branch_info.append({\n            'from_idx': i,\n            'to_idx': j,\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'g': g,\n            'b': b,\n            'b_sh_i': b_sh_i,\n            'b_sh_j': b_sh_j,\n            'T_real': T_real,\n            'T_imag': T_imag,\n            'T_mag_sq': T_mag_sq,\n            'rate': rate\n        })\n\n    # Find reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = int(np.where(bus_type == 3)[0][0])\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Voltage: V = Vr + j*Vi for each bus\n    Vr = MX.sym('Vr', n_bus)\n    Vi = MX.sym('Vi', n_bus)\n\n    # Generator power: S = Pg + j*Qg\n    Pg = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Qg = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        obj += cost_c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + cost_c1[k] * Pg[k] + cost_c0[k]\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0 (Vi[ref] = 0 since V[ref] = Vr[ref] + 0j)\n    g.append(Vi[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0)\n    ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Net generation at this bus\n        Pg_net = 0\n        Qg_net = 0\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n                Pg_net += Pg[k]\n                Qg_net += Qg[k]\n\n        # Power injection calculation\n        # S_i = V_i * conj(I_i) = V_i * conj(sum_j Y_ij * V_j)\n        # P_i = sum_j (Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) + Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n        # Q_i = sum_j (Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) - Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n\n        Pi_calc = 0\n        Qi_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if abs(G[i, j]) > 1e-10 or abs(B[i, j]) > 1e-10:\n                Pi_calc += Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) + Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n                Qi_calc += Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) - Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n\n        # Power balance: generation - load = injection\n        g.append(Pg_net - Pd[i] - Pi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Qg_net - Qd[i] - Qi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Vm_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        h.append(Vm_sq)\n        lbh.append(Vm_min[i]**2)\n        ubh.append(Vm_max[i]**2)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        h.append(Pg[k])\n        lbh.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Pg_max[k])\n\n        h.append(Qg[k])\n        lbh.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branch_info:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        g_br = br['g']\n        b_br = br['b']\n        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n        T_real = br['T_real']\n        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n        rate = br['rate']\n\n        if rate > 0:\n            # From side flow: S_ij = (y + j*b_sh_i)* * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2 - y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n            # Real part of (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*b_sh_i becomes g when conjugated\n            # So (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*(b + b_sh_i)\n\n            Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n\n            # First term: (g - j*(b + b_sh_i)) * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2\n            P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n\n            # Second term: -y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n            # = -(g - j*b) * (Vr[i] + j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] - j*Vi[j]) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n            # First compute V_i * conj(V_j) = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j])\n            ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n            ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n\n            # -y* * (ViVj) = -(g - j*b) * (ViVj_real + j*ViVj_imag)\n            #              = -(g*ViVj_real + b*ViVj_imag) - j*(g*ViVj_imag - b*ViVj_real)\n            neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n            neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n\n            # Divide by T = T_real + j*T_imag\n            # (a + j*b) / (c + j*d) = [(a*c + b*d) + j*(b*c - a*d)] / (c^2 + d^2)\n            P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n            Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n            Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n            # To side flow: S_ji = (y + j*b_sh_j)* * |V_j|^2 - y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n            Vm_j_sq = Vr[j]**2 + Vi[j]**2\n\n            # First term\n            P_term1_ji = g_br * Vm_j_sq\n            Q_term1_ji = -(b_br + b_sh_j) * Vm_j_sq\n\n            # Second term: -y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n            # conj(V_i) * V_j = (Vr[i] - j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] + j*Vi[j])\n            #                 = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vr[i]*Vi[j] - Vi[i]*Vr[j])\n            VjVi_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n            VjVi_imag = -Vi[i]*Vr[j] + Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n\n            # -y* * (VjVi)\n            neg_ystar_VjVi_real = -(g_br*VjVi_real + b_br*VjVi_imag)\n            neg_ystar_VjVi_imag = -(g_br*VjVi_imag - b_br*VjVi_real)\n\n            # Divide by conj(T) = T_real - j*T_imag\n            P_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_real - neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_real + neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n            Pji = P_term1_ji + P_term2_ji\n            Qji = Q_term1_ji + Q_term2_ji\n\n            Sji_sq = Pji**2 + Qji**2\n            h.append(Sji_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n    # Stack all variables\n    x = vertcat(Vr, Vi, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # Build NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h)\n    }\n\n    # Bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage bounds (wide bounds, actual limits enforced via constraints)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vr\n        ubx.append(2)\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vi\n        ubx.append(2)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        lbx.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Pg_max[k])\n        lbx.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    bounds = {\n        'lbx': lbx,\n        'ubx': ubx,\n        'lbg': lbg + lbh,\n        'ubg': ubg + ubh\n    }\n\n    # Initial guess (flat start with 1.0 p.u. voltage)\n    x0 = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(1.0)  # Vr\n        x0.append(0.0)  # Vi\n\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        # Start with proportional dispatch based on load\n        total_load_p = np.sum(Pd)\n        total_capacity = np.sum(Pg_max)\n        if total_capacity > 0:\n            pg_init = (Pg_min[k] + Pg_max[k]) / 2\n        else:\n            pg_init = Pg_min[k]\n        x0.append(pg_init)\n        x0.append(0.0)\n\n    return nlp, bounds, x0, {\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n        'branch_info': branch_info,\n        'G': G,\n        'B': B,\n        'Pd': Pd,\n        'Qd': Qd,\n        'bus_num_to_idx': bus_num_to_idx\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Building AC OPF model...\")\n    nlp, bounds, x0, info = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {info['n_bus']} buses, {info['n_gen']} generators, {info['n_branch']} branches\")\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes'\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"Solving AC OPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=bounds['lbx'], ubx=bounds['ubx'],\n                 lbg=bounds['lbg'], ubg=bounds['ubg'])\n\n    return sol, info\n\ndef extract_solution(sol, info):\n    \"\"\"Extract and format the solution\"\"\"\n\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    n_bus = info['n_bus']\n    n_gen = info['n_gen']\n    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n\n    # Extract voltages\n    Vr = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Vi = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Vm = np.sqrt(Vr**2 + Vi**2)\n    Va = np.arctan2(Vi, Vr) * 180 / np.pi\n\n    # Extract generator dispatch\n    Pg = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for br in info['branch_info']:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        g_br = br['g']\n        b_br = br['b']\n        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n        T_real = br['T_real']\n        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n\n        # From side flow\n        Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n\n        ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n        ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n        neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n        neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n        P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n        Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n        Sij_MVA = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # Most loaded branches (top 10)\n    branch_flows = solution['branch_flows']\n    branch_flows_sorted = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n\n    most_loaded_branches = []\n    for br in branch_flows_sorted[:10]:\n        most_loaded_branches.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br['from_bus']),\n            'to_bus': int(br['to_bus']),\n            'loading_pct': float(br['loading_pct']),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(br['Sij_MVA']),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(br['Sji_MVA']),\n            'limit_MVA': float(br['rate_MVA'])\n        })\n\n    # Resistance\n    88→        x = branch_data[k, 3]  # Reactance\n    89→        b_ch = branch_data[k, 4]  # Total line charging susceptance\n    90→        tap = branch_data[k, 8]  # Transformer tap ratio\n    91→        shift = branch_data[k, 9]  # Transformer phase shift (degrees)\n    92→        rate = branch_data[k, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating\n    93→\n    94→        if tap == 0:\n    95→            tap = 1.0\n    96→\n    97→        # Branch series admittance y = g + j*b\n    98→        z_mag_sq = r**2 + x**2\n    99→        if z_mag_sq > 1e-10:\n   100→            g = r / z_mag_sq\n   101→            b = -x / z_mag_sq\n   102→        else:\n   103→            g = 0\n   104→            b = 0\n   105→\n   106→        # Transformer complex ratio T = tap * e^(j*shift)\n   107→        # T = tap * (cos(shift) + j*sin(shift))\n   108→        shift_rad = shift * np.pi / 180\n   109→        T_real = tap * np.cos(shift_rad)\n   110→        T_imag = tap * np.sin(shift_rad)\n   111→        T_mag_sq = tap**2\n   112→\n   113→        # Shunt charging admittances\n   114→        b_sh_i = b_ch / 2\n   115→        b_sh_j = b_ch / 2\n   116→\n   117→        # Add to admittance matrix\n   118→        # From-side self admittance: (y + j*b_sh_i) / |T|^2\n   119→        G[i, i] += g / T_mag_sq\n   120→        B[i, i] += (b + b_sh_i) / T_mag_sq\n   121→\n   122→        # To-side self admittance: y + j*b_sh_j\n   123→        G[j, j] += g\n   124→        B[j, j] += b + b_sh_j\n   125→\n   126→        # Mutual admittances: -y / T*  and  -y / T\n   127→        # -y / T* = -(g + j*b) / (T_real - j*T_imag)\n   128→        #         = -(g + j*b) * (T_real + j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n   129→        #         = (-(g*T_real - b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real - g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n   130→        G[i, j] += -(g*T_real - b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   131→        B[i, j] += -(b*T_real + g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   132→\n   133→        # -y / T = -(g + j*b) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n   134→        #        = -(g + j*b) * (T_real - j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n   135→        #        = (-(g*T_real + b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real + g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n   136→        G[j, i] += -(g*T_real + b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   137→        B[j, i] += -(b*T_real - g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   138→\n   139→        branch_info.append({\n   140→            'from_idx': i,\n   141→            'to_idx': j,\n   142→            'from_bus': from_bus,\n   143→            'to_bus': to_bus,\n   144→            'g': g,\n   145→            'b': b,\n   146→            'b_sh_i': b_sh_i,\n   147→            'b_sh_j': b_sh_j,\n   148→            'T_real': T_real,\n   149→            'T_imag': T_imag,\n   150→            'T_mag_sq': T_mag_sq,\n   151→            'rate': rate\n   152→        })\n   153→\n   154→    # Find reference bus\n   155→    ref_bus_idx = int(np.where(bus_type == 3)[0][0])\n   156→\n   157→    # Decision variables\n   158→    # Voltage: V = Vr + j*Vi for each bus\n   159→    Vr = MX.sym('Vr', n_bus)\n   160→    Vi = MX.sym('Vi', n_bus)\n   161→\n   162→    # Generator power: S = Pg + j*Qg\n   163→    Pg = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n   164→    Qg = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n   165→\n   166→    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n   167→    obj = 0\n   168→    for k in range(n_gen):\n   169→        obj += cost_c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + cost_c1[k] * Pg[k] + cost_c0[k]\n   170→\n   171→    # Constraints\n   172→    g = []  # Equality constraints\n   173→    lbg = []\n   174→    ubg = []\n   175→    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n   176→    lbh = []\n   177→    ubh = []\n   178→\n   179→    # Reference bus angle = 0 (Vi[ref] = 0 since V[ref] = Vr[ref] + 0j)\n   180→    g.append(Vi[ref_bus_idx])\n   181→    lbg.append(0)\n   182→    ubg.append(0)\n   183→\n   184→    # Power balance at each bus\n   185→    for i in range(n_bus):\n   186→        # Net generation at this bus\n   187→        Pg_net = 0\n   188→        Qg_net = 0\n   189→        for k in range(n_gen):\n   190→            if gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n   191→                Pg_net += Pg[k]\n   192→                Qg_net += Qg[k]\n   193→\n   194→        # Power injection calculation\n   195→        # S_i = V_i * conj(I_i) = V_i * conj(sum_j Y_ij * V_j)\n   196→        # P_i = sum_j (Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) + Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n   197→        # Q_i = sum_j (Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) - Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n   198→\n   199→        Pi_calc = 0\n   200→        Qi_calc = 0\n   201→\n   202→        for j in range(n_bus):\n   203→            if abs(G[i, j]) > 1e-10 or abs(B[i, j]) > 1e-10:\n   204→                Pi_calc += Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) + Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n   205→                Qi_calc += Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) - Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n   206→\n   207→        # Power balance: generation - load = injection\n   208→        g.append(Pg_net - Pd[i] - Pi_calc)\n   209→        lbg.append(0)\n   210→        ubg.append(0)\n   211→\n   212→        g.append(Qg_net - Qd[i] - Qi_calc)\n   213→        lbg.append(0)\n   214→        ubg.append(0)\n   215→\n   216→    # Voltage magnitude limits\n   217→    for i in range(n_bus):\n   218→        Vm_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n   219→        h.append(Vm_sq)\n   220→        lbh.append(Vm_min[i]**2)\n   221→        ubh.append(Vm_max[i]**2)\n   222→\n   223→    # Generator limits\n   224→    for k in range(n_gen):\n   225→        h.append(Pg[k])\n   226→        lbh.append(Pg_min[k])\n   227→        ubh.append(Pg_max[k])\n   228→\n   229→        h.append(Qg[k])\n   230→        lbh.append(Qg_min[k])\n   231→        ubh.append(Qg_max[k])\n   232→\n   233→    # Branch flow limits\n   234→    for br in branch_info:\n   235→        i = br['from_idx']\n   236→        j = br['to_idx']\n   237→        g_br = br['g']\n   238→        b_br = br['b']\n   239→        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n   240→        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n   241→        T_real = br['T_real']\n   242→        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n   243→        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n   244→        rate = br['rate']\n   245→\n   246→        if rate > 0:\n   247→            # From side flow: S_ij = (y + j*b_sh_i)* * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2 - y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n   248→            # Real part of (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*b_sh_i becomes g when conjugated\n   249→            # So (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*(b + b_sh_i)\n   250→\n   251→            Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n   252→\n   253→            # First term: (g - j*(b + b_sh_i)) * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2\n   254→            P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n   255→            Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n   256→\n   257→            # Second term: -y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n   258→            # = -(g - j*b) * (Vr[i] + j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] - j*Vi[j]) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n   259→            # First compute V_i * conj(V_j) = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j])\n   260→            ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n   261→            ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n   262→\n   263→            # -y* * (ViVj) = -(g - j*b) * (ViVj_real + j*ViVj_imag)\n   264→            #              = -(g*ViVj_real + b*ViVj_imag) - j*(g*ViVj_imag - b*ViVj_real)\n   265→            neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n   266→            neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n   267→\n   268→            # Divide by T = T_real + j*T_imag\n   269→            # (a + j*b) / (c + j*d) = [(a*c + b*d) + j*(b*c - a*d)] / (c^2 + d^2)\n   270→            P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   271→            Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   272→\n   273→            Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n   274→            Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n   275→\n   276→            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n   277→            h.append(Sij_sq)\n   278→            lbh.append(0)\n   279→            ubh.append(rate**2)\n   280→\n   281→            # To side flow: S_ji = (y + j*b_sh_j)* * |V_j|^2 - y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n   282→            Vm_j_sq = Vr[j]**2 + Vi[j]**2\n   283→\n   284→            # First term\n   285→            P_term1_ji = g_br * Vm_j_sq\n   286→            Q_term1_ji = -(b_br + b_sh_j) * Vm_j_sq\n   287→\n   288→            # Second term: -y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n   289→            # conj(V_i) * V_j = (Vr[i] - j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] + j*Vi[j])\n   290→            #                 = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vr[i]*Vi[j] - Vi[i]*Vr[j])\n   291→            VjVi_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n   292→            VjVi_imag = -Vi[i]*Vr[j] + Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n   293→\n   294→            # -y* * (VjVi)\n   295→            neg_ystar_VjVi_real = -(g_br*VjVi_real + b_br*VjVi_imag)\n   296→            neg_ystar_VjVi_imag = -(g_br*VjVi_imag - b_br*VjVi_real)\n   297→\n   298→            # Divide by conj(T) = T_real - j*T_imag\n   299→            P_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_real - neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   300→            Q_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_real + neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   301→\n   302→            Pji = P_term1_ji + P_term2_ji\n   303→            Qji = Q_term1_ji + Q_term2_ji\n   304→\n   305→            Sji_sq = Pji**2 + Qji**2\n   306→            h.append(Sji_sq)\n   307→            lbh.append(0)\n   308→            ubh.append(rate**2)\n   309→\n   310→    # Stack all variables\n   311→    x = vertcat(Vr, Vi, Pg, Qg)\n   312→\n   313→    # Build NLP\n   314→    nlp = {\n   315→        'x': x,\n   316→        'f': obj,\n   317→        'g': vertcat(*g, *h)\n   318→    }\n   319→\n   320→    # Bounds\n   321→    lbx = []\n   322→    ubx = []\n   323→\n   324→    # Voltage bounds (wide bounds, actual limits enforced via constraints)\n   325→    for i in range(n_bus):\n   326→        lbx.append(-2)  # Vr\n   327→        ubx.append(2)\n   328→        lbx.append(-2)  # Vi\n   329→        ubx.append(2)\n   330→\n   331→    # Generator bounds\n   332→    for k in range(n_gen):\n   333→        lbx.append(Pg_min[k])\n   334→        ubx.append(Pg_max[k])\n   335→        lbx.append(Qg_min[k])\n   336→        ubx.append(Qg_max[k])\n   337→\n   338→    bounds = {\n   339→        'lbx': lbx,\n   340→        'ubx': ubx,\n   341→        'lbg': lbg + lbh,\n   342→        'ubg': ubg + ubh\n   343→    }\n   344→\n   345→    # Initial guess (flat start with 1.0 p.u. voltage)\n   346→    x0 = []\n   347→    for i in range(n_bus):\n   348→        x0.append(1.0)  # Vr\n   349→        x0.append(0.0)  # Vi\n   350→\n   351→    for k in range(n_gen):\n   352→        # Start with proportional dispatch based on load\n   353→        total_load_p = np.sum(Pd)\n   354→        total_capacity = np.sum(Pg_max)\n   355→        if total_capacity > 0:\n   356→            pg_init = (Pg_min[k] + Pg_max[k]) / 2\n   357→        else:\n   358→            pg_init = Pg_min[k]\n   359→        x0.append(pg_init)\n   360→        x0.append(0.0)\n   361→\n   362→    return nlp, bounds, x0, {\n   363→        'n_bus': n_bus,\n   364→        'n_gen': n_gen,\n   365→        'n_branch': n_branch,\n   366→        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n   367→        'bus_data': bus_data,\n   368→        'gen_data': gen_data,\n   369→        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n   370→        'branch_info': branch_info,\n   371→        'G': G,\n   372→        'B': B,\n   373→        'Pd': Pd,\n   374→        'Qd': Qd,\n   375→        'bus_num_to_idx': bus_num_to_idx\n   376→    }\n   377→\n   378→def solve_acopf(network):\n   379→    \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n   380→\n   381→    print(\"Building AC OPF model...\")\n   382→    nlp, bounds, x0, info = build_acopf_model(network)\n   383→\n   384→    print(f\"Problem size: {info['n_bus']} buses, {info['n_gen']} generators, {info['n_branch']} branches\")\n   385→\n   386→    # Create IPOPT solver\n   387→    opts = {\n   388→        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n   389→        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n   390→        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n   391→        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n   392→        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n   393→        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes'\n   394→    }\n   395→\n   396→    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n   397→\n   398→    print(\"Solving AC OPF...\")\n   399→    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=bounds['lbx'], ubx=bounds['ubx'],\n   400→                 lbg=bounds['lbg'], ubg=bounds['ubg'])\n   401→\n   402→    return sol, info\n   403→\n   404→def extract_solution(sol, info):\n   405→    \"\"\"Extract and format the solution\"\"\"\n   406→\n   407→    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n   408→\n   409→    n_bus = info['n_bus']\n   410→    n_gen = info['n_gen']\n   411→    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n   412→\n   413→    # Extract voltages\n   414→    Vr = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n   415→    Vi = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n   416→    Vm = np.sqrt(Vr**2 + Vi**2)\n   417→    Va = np.arctan2(Vi, Vr) * 180 / np.pi\n   418→\n   419→    # Extract generator dispatch\n   420→    Pg = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n   421→    Qg = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n   422→\n   423→    # Calculate branch flows\n   424→    branch_flows = []\n   425→    for br in info['branch_info']:\n   426→        i = br['from_idx']\n   427→        j = br['to_idx']\n   428→        g_br = br['g']\n   429→        b_br = br['b']\n   430→        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n   431→        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n   432→        T_real = br['T_real']\n   433→        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n   434→        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n   435→\n   436→        # From side flow\n   437→        Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n   438→        P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n   439→        Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n   440→\n   441→        ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n   442→        ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n   443→        neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n   444→        neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n   445→        P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   446→        Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   447→\n   448→        Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n   449→        Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n   450→        Sij_MVA = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2) * baseMVA\n   451→\n   452→        # To side flow\n   453→        Vm_j_sq = Vr[j]**2 + Vi[j]**2\n   454→        P_term1_ji = g_br * Vm_j_sq\n   455→        Q_term1_ji = -(b_br + b_sh_j) * Vm_j_sq\n   456→\n   457→        VjVi_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n   458→        VjVi_imag = -Vi[i]*Vr[j] + Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n   459→        neg_ystar_VjVi_real = -(g_br*VjVi_real + b_br*VjVi_imag)\n   460→        neg_ystar_VjVi_imag = -(g_br*VjVi_imag - b_br*VjVi_real)\n   461→        P_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_real - neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   462→        Q_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_real + neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n   463→\n   464→        Pji = P_term1_ji + P_term2_ji\n   465→        Qji = Q_term1_ji + Q_term2_ji\n   466→        Sji_MVA = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2) * baseMVA\n   467→\n   468→        rate_MVA = br['rate'] * baseMVA\n   469→        loading_pct = (max(Sij_MVA, Sji_MVA) / rate_MVA * 100) if rate_MVA > 0 else 0\n   470→\n   471→        branch_flows.append({\n   472→            'from_bus': br['from_bus'],\n   473→            'to_bus': br['to_bus'],\n   474→            'Sij_MVA': float(Sij_MVA),\n   475→            'Sji_MVA': float(Sji_MVA),\n   476→            'rate_MVA': float(rate_MVA),\n   477→            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct)\n   478→        })\n   479→\n   480→    return {\n   481→        'Vm': Vm,\n   482→        'Va': Va,\n   483→        'Pg': Pg,\n   484→        'Qg': Qg,\n   485→        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n   486→        'objective': float(sol['f'])\n   487→    }\n   488→\n   489→def generate_report(sol, info, network):\n   490→    \"\"\"Generate the JSON report\"\"\"\n   491→\n   492→    solution = extract_solution(sol, info)\n   493→    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n   494→\n   495→    # Calculate totals\n   496→    Pg_MW = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA\n   497→    Qg_MVAr = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA\n   498→\n   499→    total_gen_MW = float(np.sum(Pg_MW))\n   500→    total_gen_MVAr = float(np.sum(Qg_MVAr))\n   501→\n   502→    total_load_MW = float(np.sum(info['Pd']) * baseMVA)\n   503→    total_load_MVAr = float(np.sum(info['Qd']) * baseMVA)\n   504→\n   505→    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n   506→\n   507→    # Generator data\n   508→    generators = []\n   509→    for k in range(info['n_gen']):\n   510→        bus_idx = info['gen_bus_idx'][k]\n   511→        bus_num = int(info['bus_data'][bus_idx, 0])\n   512→        gen_data_row = info['gen_data'][k]\n   513→\n   514→        generators.append({\n   515→            'id': k + 1,\n   516→            'bus': bus_num,\n   517→            'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[k]),\n   518→            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[k]),\n   519→            'pmin_MW': float(gen_data_row[9]),\n   520→            'pmax_MW': float(gen_data_row[8]),\n   521→            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen_data_row[4]),\n   522→            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen_data_row[3])\n   523→        })\n   524→\n   525→    # Bus data\n   526→    buses = []\n   527→    for i in range(info['n_bus']):\n   528→        bus_num = int(info['bus_data'][i, 0])\n   529→        buses.append({\n   530→            'id': bus_num,\n   531→            'vm_pu': float(solution['Vm'][i]),\n   532→            'va_deg': float(solution['Va'][i]),\n   533→            'vmin_pu': float(info['bus_data'][i, 12]),\n   534→            'vmax_pu': float(info['bus_data'][i, 11])\n   535→        })\n   536→\n   537→    # Feasibility check\n   553→    max_voltage_violation = 0\n   554→    for i in range(info['n_bus']):\n   555→        Vm = solution['Vm'][i]\n   556→        Vmin = info['bus_data'][i, 12]\n   557→        Vmax = info['bus_data'][i, 11]\n   558→        violation = max(0, Vmin - Vm, Vm - Vmax)\n   559→        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n   560→\n   561→    max_branch_overload = 0\n   562→    for br in branch_flows:\n   563→        if br['rate_MVA'] > 0:\n   564→            overload = max(0, br['Sij_MVA'] - br['rate_MVA'], br['Sji_MVA'] - br['rate_MVA'])\n   565→            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n   566→\n   567→    # Get solver status\n   568→    stats = sol['solver'].stats()\n   569→    solver_status = 'optimal' if stats['success'] else 'failed'\n   570→\n   571→    report = {\n   572→        'summary': {\n   573→            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['objective']),\n   574→            'total_load_MW': total_load_MW,\n   575→            'total_load_MVAr': total_load_MVAr,\n   576→            'total_generation_MW': total_gen_MW,\n   577→            'total_generation_MVAr': total_gen_MVAr,\n   578→            'total_losses_MW': total_losses_MW,\n   579→            'solver_status': solver_status\n   580→        },\n   581→        'generators': generators,\n   582→        'buses': buses,\n   583→        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded_branches,\n   584→        'feasibility_check': {\n   585→            'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,\n   586→            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.001,\n   587→            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n   588→            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n   589→        }\n   590→    }\n   591→\n   592→    return report\n   593→\n   594→def main():\n   595→    \"\"\"Main function\"\"\"\n   596→\n   597→    # Load network data\n   598→    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n   599→    network = load_network_data('network.json')\n   600→\n   601→    # Solve AC OPF\n   602→    sol, info = solve_acopf(network)\n   603→\n   604→    # Generate report\n   605→    print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n   606→    report = generate_report(sol, info, network)\n   607→\n   608→    # Save report\n   609→    with open('report.json', 'w') as f:\n   610→        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n   611→\n   612→    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n   613→    print(\"SOLUTION SUMMARY\")\n   614→    print(\"=\"*60)\n   615→    print(f\"Total Cost: ${report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hour\")\n   616→    print(f\"Total Generation: {report['summary']['total_generation_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n   617→    print(f\"Total Load: {report['summary']['total_load_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n   618→    print(f\"Total Losses: {report['summary']['total_losses_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n   619→    print(f\"Solver Status: {report['summary']['solver_status']}\")\n   620→    print(\"=\"*60)\n   621→    print(f\"\\nReport saved to report.json\")\n   622→\n   623→if __name__ == '__main__':\n   624→    main()\n   625→"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"eb5b3d47-1aee-4716-b32b-a55141fed861","uuid":"d2adcbba-31f9-425b-bee4-17404987bdc7","tool_use_result":{"type":"update","filePath":"/root/solve_acopf.py","content":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver for Independent System Operator\nImplements the complex number based mathematical model from math-model.md\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom casadi import *\n\ndef load_network_data(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load and parse the network JSON data\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data according to MATPOWER format\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': bus_data,\n        'gen': gen_data,\n        'branch': branch_data,\n        'gencost': gencost_data\n    }\n\ndef build_acopf_model(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC OPF optimization model using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network['bus']\n    gen_data = network['gen']\n    branch_data = network['branch']\n    gencost_data = network['gencost']\n\n    n_bus = bus_data.shape[0]\n    n_gen = gen_data.shape[0]\n    n_branch = branch_data.shape[0]\n\n    # Build bus index mapping (bus number -> index)\n    bus_num_to_idx = {int(bus_data[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Extract bus data (MATPOWER format columns)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1]  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    Pd = bus_data[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Real power demand (p.u.)\n    Qd = bus_data[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Reactive power demand (p.u.)\n    Gs = bus_data[:, 4] / baseMVA  # Shunt conductance\n    Bs = bus_data[:, 5] / baseMVA  # Shunt susceptance\n    Vm_min = bus_data[:, 12]  # Min voltage magnitude\n    Vm_max = bus_data[:, 11]  # Max voltage magnitude\n\n    # Extract generator data\n    gen_bus_num = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_num_to_idx[num] for num in gen_bus_num]\n    Pg_min = gen_data[:, 9] / baseMVA\n    Pg_max = gen_data[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    Qg_min = gen_data[:, 4] / baseMVA\n    Qg_max = gen_data[:, 3] / baseMVA\n\n    # Extract cost data (assume quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0)\n    cost_c2 = gencost_data[:, 4] * baseMVA**2  # Convert to p.u.\n    cost_c1 = gencost_data[:, 5] * baseMVA\n    cost_c0 = gencost_data[:, 6]\n\n    # Build Ybus matrix using real and imaginary parts\n    G = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))  # Real part (conductance)\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))  # Imaginary part (susceptance)\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        G[i, i] += Gs[i]\n        B[i, i] += Bs[i]\n\n    # Build admittance matrix from branch data\n    branch_info = []\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[k, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[k, 1])\n        i = bus_num_to_idx[from_bus]\n        j = bus_num_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        r = branch_data[k, 2]  # Resistance\n        x = branch_data[k, 3]  # Reactance\n        b_ch = branch_data[k, 4]  # Total line charging susceptance\n        tap = branch_data[k, 8]  # Transformer tap ratio\n        shift = branch_data[k, 9]  # Transformer phase shift (degrees)\n        rate = branch_data[k, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Branch series admittance y = g + j*b\n        z_mag_sq = r**2 + x**2\n        if z_mag_sq > 1e-10:\n            g = r / z_mag_sq\n            b = -x / z_mag_sq\n        else:\n            g = 0\n            b = 0\n\n        # Transformer complex ratio T = tap * e^(j*shift)\n        # T = tap * (cos(shift) + j*sin(shift))\n        shift_rad = shift * np.pi / 180\n        T_real = tap * np.cos(shift_rad)\n        T_imag = tap * np.sin(shift_rad)\n        T_mag_sq = tap**2\n\n        # Shunt charging admittances\n        b_sh_i = b_ch / 2\n        b_sh_j = b_ch / 2\n\n        # Add to admittance matrix\n        # From-side self admittance: (y + j*b_sh_i) / |T|^2\n        G[i, i] += g / T_mag_sq\n        B[i, i] += (b + b_sh_i) / T_mag_sq\n\n        # To-side self admittance: y + j*b_sh_j\n        G[j, j] += g\n        B[j, j] += b + b_sh_j\n\n        # Mutual admittances: -y / T*  and  -y / T\n        # -y / T* = -(g + j*b) / (T_real - j*T_imag)\n        #         = -(g + j*b) * (T_real + j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n        #         = (-(g*T_real - b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real - g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n        G[i, j] += -(g*T_real - b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        B[i, j] += -(b*T_real + g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        # -y / T = -(g + j*b) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n        #        = -(g + j*b) * (T_real - j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n        #        = (-(g*T_real + b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real + g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n        G[j, i] += -(g*T_real + b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        B[j, i] += -(b*T_real - g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        branch_info.append({\n            'from_idx': i,\n            'to_idx': j,\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'g': g,\n            'b': b,\n            'b_sh_i': b_sh_i,\n            'b_sh_j': b_sh_j,\n            'T_real': T_real,\n            'T_imag': T_imag,\n            'T_mag_sq': T_mag_sq,\n            'rate': rate\n        })\n\n    # Find reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = int(np.where(bus_type == 3)[0][0])\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Voltage: V = Vr + j*Vi for each bus\n    Vr = MX.sym('Vr', n_bus)\n    Vi = MX.sym('Vi', n_bus)\n\n    # Generator power: S = Pg + j*Qg\n    Pg = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Qg = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        obj += cost_c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + cost_c1[k] * Pg[k] + cost_c0[k]\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0 (Vi[ref] = 0 since V[ref] = Vr[ref] + 0j)\n    g.append(Vi[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0)\n    ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Net generation at this bus\n        Pg_net = 0\n        Qg_net = 0\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n                Pg_net += Pg[k]\n                Qg_net += Qg[k]\n\n        # Power injection calculation\n        # S_i = V_i * conj(I_i) = V_i * conj(sum_j Y_ij * V_j)\n        # P_i = sum_j (Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) + Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n        # Q_i = sum_j (Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) - Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n\n        Pi_calc = 0\n        Qi_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if abs(G[i, j]) > 1e-10 or abs(B[i, j]) > 1e-10:\n                Pi_calc += Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) + Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n                Qi_calc += Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) - Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n\n        # Power balance: generation - load = injection\n        g.append(Pg_net - Pd[i] - Pi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Qg_net - Qd[i] - Qi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Vm_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        h.append(Vm_sq)\n        lbh.append(Vm_min[i]**2)\n        ubh.append(Vm_max[i]**2)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        h.append(Pg[k])\n        lbh.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Pg_max[k])\n\n        h.append(Qg[k])\n        lbh.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branch_info:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        g_br = br['g']\n        b_br = br['b']\n        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n        T_real = br['T_real']\n        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n        rate = br['rate']\n\n        if rate > 0:\n            # From side flow: S_ij = (y + j*b_sh_i)* * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2 - y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n            # Real part of (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*b_sh_i becomes g when conjugated\n            # So (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*(b + b_sh_i)\n\n            Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n\n            # First term: (g - j*(b + b_sh_i)) * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2\n            P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n\n            # Second term: -y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n            # = -(g - j*b) * (Vr[i] + j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] - j*Vi[j]) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n            # First compute V_i * conj(V_j) = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j])\n            ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n            ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n\n            # -y* * (ViVj) = -(g - j*b) * (ViVj_real + j*ViVj_imag)\n            #              = -(g*ViVj_real + b*ViVj_imag) - j*(g*ViVj_imag - b*ViVj_real)\n            neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n            neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n\n            # Divide by T = T_real + j*T_imag\n            # (a + j*b) / (c + j*d) = [(a*c + b*d) + j*(b*c - a*d)] / (c^2 + d^2)\n            P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n            Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n            Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n            # To side flow: S_ji = (y + j*b_sh_j)* * |V_j|^2 - y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n            Vm_j_sq = Vr[j]**2 + Vi[j]**2\n\n            # First term\n            P_term1_ji = g_br * Vm_j_sq\n            Q_term1_ji = -(b_br + b_sh_j) * Vm_j_sq\n\n            # Second term: -y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n            # conj(V_i) * V_j = (Vr[i] - j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] + j*Vi[j])\n            #                 = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vr[i]*Vi[j] - Vi[i]*Vr[j])\n            VjVi_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n            VjVi_imag = -Vi[i]*Vr[j] + Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n\n            # -y* * (VjVi)\n            neg_ystar_VjVi_real = -(g_br*VjVi_real + b_br*VjVi_imag)\n            neg_ystar_VjVi_imag = -(g_br*VjVi_imag - b_br*VjVi_real)\n\n            # Divide by conj(T) = T_real - j*T_imag\n            P_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_real - neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_real + neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n            Pji = P_term1_ji + P_term2_ji\n            Qji = Q_term1_ji + Q_term2_ji\n\n            Sji_sq = Pji**2 + Qji**2\n            h.append(Sji_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n    # Stack all variables\n    x = vertcat(Vr, Vi, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # Build NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h)\n    }\n\n    # Bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage bounds (wide bounds, actual limits enforced via constraints)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vr\n        ubx.append(2)\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vi\n        ubx.append(2)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        lbx.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Pg_max[k])\n        lbx.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    bounds = {\n        'lbx': lbx,\n        'ubx': ubx,\n        'lbg': lbg + lbh,\n        'ubg': ubg + ubh\n    }\n\n    # Initial guess (flat start with 1.0 p.u. voltage)\n    x0 = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(1.0)  # Vr\n        x0.append(0.0)  # Vi\n\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        # Start with proportional dispatch based on load\n        total_load_p = np.sum(Pd)\n        total_capacity = np.sum(Pg_max)\n        if total_capacity > 0:\n            pg_init = (Pg_min[k] + Pg_max[k]) / 2\n        else:\n            pg_init = Pg_min[k]\n        x0.append(pg_init)\n        x0.append(0.0)\n\n    return nlp, bounds, x0, {\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n        'branch_info': branch_info,\n        'G': G,\n        'B': B,\n        'Pd': Pd,\n        'Qd': Qd,\n        'bus_num_to_idx': bus_num_to_idx\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Building AC OPF model...\")\n    nlp, bounds, x0, info = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {info['n_bus']} buses, {info['n_gen']} generators, {info['n_branch']} branches\")\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes'\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"Solving AC OPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=bounds['lbx'], ubx=bounds['ubx'],\n                 lbg=bounds['lbg'], ubg=bounds['ubg'])\n\n    return sol, info\n\ndef extract_solution(sol, info):\n    \"\"\"Extract and format the solution\"\"\"\n\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    n_bus = info['n_bus']\n    n_gen = info['n_gen']\n    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n\n    # Extract voltages\n    Vr = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Vi = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Vm = np.sqrt(Vr**2 + Vi**2)\n    Va = np.arctan2(Vi, Vr) * 180 / np.pi\n\n    # Extract generator dispatch\n    Pg = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for br in info['branch_info']:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        g_br = br['g']\n        b_br = br['b']\n        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n        T_real = br['T_real']\n        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n\n        # From side flow\n        Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n\n        ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n        ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n        neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n        neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n        P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n        Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n        Sij_MVA = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # To side flow\n        Vm_j_sq = Vr[j]**2 + Vi[j]**2\n        P_term1_ji = g_br * Vm_j_sq\n        Q_term1_ji = -(b_br + b_sh_j) * Vm_j_sq\n\n        VjVi_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n        VjVi_imag = -Vi[i]*Vr[j] + Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n        neg_ystar_VjVi_real = -(g_br*VjVi_real + b_br*VjVi_imag)\n        neg_ystar_VjVi_imag = -(g_br*VjVi_imag - b_br*VjVi_real)\n        P_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_real - neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_real + neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        Pji = P_term1_ji + P_term2_ji\n        Qji = Q_term1_ji + Q_term2_ji\n        Sji_MVA = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2) * baseMVA\n\n        rate_MVA = br['rate'] * baseMVA\n        loading_pct = (max(Sij_MVA, Sji_MVA) / rate_MVA * 100) if rate_MVA > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': br['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': br['to_bus'],\n            'Sij_MVA': float(Sij_MVA),\n            'Sji_MVA': float(Sji_MVA),\n            'rate_MVA': float(rate_MVA),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm,\n        'Va': Va,\n        'Pg': Pg,\n        'Qg': Qg,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'objective': float(sol['f'])\n    }\n\ndef generate_report(sol, info, network):\n    \"\"\"Generate the JSON report\"\"\"\n\n    solution = extract_solution(sol, info)\n    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    Pg_MW = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA\n    Qg_MVAr = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA\n\n    total_gen_MW = float(np.sum(Pg_MW))\n    total_gen_MVAr = float(np.sum(Qg_MVAr))\n\n    total_load_MW = float(np.sum(info['Pd']) * baseMVA)\n    total_load_MVAr = float(np.sum(info['Qd']) * baseMVA)\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Generator data\n    generators = []\n    for k in range(info['n_gen']):\n        bus_idx = info['gen_bus_idx'][k]\n        bus_num = int(info['bus_data'][bus_idx, 0])\n        gen_data_row = info['gen_data'][k]\n\n        generators.append({\n            'id': k + 1,\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[k]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[k]),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen_data_row[9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen_data_row[8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen_data_row[4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen_data_row[3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus data\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(info['n_bus']):\n        bus_num = int(info['bus_data'][i, 0])\n        buses.append({\n            'id': bus_num,\n            'vm_pu': float(solution['Vm'][i]),\n            'va_deg': float(solution['Va'][i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(info['bus_data'][i, 12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(info['bus_data'][i, 11])\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches (top 10)\n    branch_flows = solution['branch_flows']\n    branch_flows_sorted = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n\n    most_loaded_branches = []\n    for br in branch_flows_sorted[:10]:\n        most_loaded_branches.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br['from_bus']),\n            'to_bus': int(br['to_bus']),\n            'loading_pct': float(br['loading_pct']),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(br['Sij_MVA']),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(br['Sji_MVA']),\n            'limit_MVA': float(br['rate_MVA'])\n        })\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    for i in range(info['n_bus']):\n        Vm = solution['Vm'][i]\n        Vmin = info['bus_data'][i, 12]\n        Vmax = info['bus_data'][i, 11]\n        violation = max(0, Vmin - Vm, Vm - Vmax)\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n    for br in branch_flows:\n        if br['rate_MVA'] > 0:\n            overload = max(0, br['Sij_MVA'] - br['rate_MVA'], br['Sji_MVA'] - br['rate_MVA'])\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = sol['solver'].stats()\n    solver_status = 'optimal' if stats['success'] else 'failed'\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['objective']),\n            'total_load_MW': total_load_MW,\n            'total_load_MVAr': total_load_MVAr,\n            'total_generation_MW': total_gen_MW,\n            'total_generation_MVAr': total_gen_MVAr,\n            'total_losses_MW': total_losses_MW,\n            'solver_status': solver_status\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded_branches,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.001,\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function\"\"\"\n\n    # Parse bus data according to MATPOWER format\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': bus_data,\n        'gen': gen_data,\n        'branch': branch_data,\n        'gencost': gencost_data\n    }\n\ndef build_acopf_model(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC OPF optimization model using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network['bus']\n    gen_data = network['gen']\n    branch_data = network['branch']\n    gencost_data = network['gencost']\n\n    n_bus = bus_data.shape[0]\n    n_gen = gen_data.shape[0]\n    n_branch = branch_data.shape[0]\n\n    # Build bus index mapping (bus number -> index)\n    bus_num_to_idx = {int(bus_data[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Extract bus data (MATPOWER format columns)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1]  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    Pd = bus_data[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Real power demand (p.u.)\n    Qd = bus_data[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Reactive power demand (p.u.)\n    Vm_min = bus_data[:, 12]  # Min voltage magnitude\n    Vm_max = bus_data[:, 11]  # Max voltage magnitude\n\n    # Extract generator data\n    gen_bus_num = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_num_to_idx[num] for num in gen_bus_num]\n    Pg_min = gen_data[:, 9] / baseMVA\n    Pg_max = gen_data[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    Qg_min = gen_data[:, 4] / baseMVA\n    Qg_max = gen_data[:, 3] / baseMVA\n\n    # Extract cost data (assume quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0)\n    cost_c2 = gencost_data[:, 4] * baseMVA**2  # Convert to p.u.\n    cost_c1 = gencost_data[:, 5] * baseMVA\n    cost_c0 = gencost_data[:, 6]\n\n    # Build Ybus matrix\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    Gs = bus_data[:, 4] / baseMVA  # Shunt conductance\n    Bs = bus_data[:, 5] / baseMVA  # Shunt susceptance\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Ybus[i, i] += complex(Gs[i], Bs[i])\n\n    # Build admittance matrix from branch data\n    branch_info = []\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[k, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[k, 1])\n        i = bus_num_to_idx[from_bus]\n        j = bus_num_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        r = branch_data[k, 2]  # Resistance\n        x = branch_data[k, 3]  # Reactance\n        b = branch_data[k, 4]  # Total line charging susceptance\n        tap = branch_data[k, 8]  # Transformer tap ratio\n        shift = branch_data[k, 9]  # Transformer phase shift (degrees)\n        rate = branch_data[k, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Branch admittance\n        y = 1 / complex(r, x) if (r**2 + x**2) > 0 else 0\n\n        # Transformer complex ratio (using numpy for complex numbers)\n        T = tap * np.exp(1j * shift * np.pi / 180)\n\n        # Shunt admittances (half charging on each side)\n        yc_i = 1j * b / 2\n        yc_j = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Add to Ybus\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y + yc_i) / abs(T)**2\n        Ybus[j, j] += y + yc_j\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y / np.conj(T)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y / T\n\n        branch_info.append({\n            'from_idx': i,\n            'to_idx': j,\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'y': y,\n            'yc_i': yc_i,\n            'yc_j': yc_j,\n            'T': T,\n            'rate': rate\n        })\n\n    # Find reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = int(np.where(bus_type == 3)[0][0])\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Complex voltage V = Vr + j*Vi for each bus\n    Vr = MX.sym('Vr', n_bus)\n    Vi = MX.sym('Vi', n_bus)\n\n    # Generator complex power S = Pg + j*Qg\n    Pg = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Qg = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        obj += cost_c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + cost_c1[k] * Pg[k] + cost_c0[k]\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0\n    g.append(Vi[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0)\n    ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Calculate net injection from generators at this bus\n        Pg_net = 0\n        Qg_net = 0\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n                Pg_net += Pg[k]\n                Qg_net += Qg[k]\n\n        # Calculate power flow from this bus\n        Pi_calc = 0\n        Qi_calc = 0\n        V_i = Vr[i] + 1j * Vi[i]\n        Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if abs(Ybus[i, j]) > 1e-10:\n                V_j = Vr[j] + 1j * Vi[j]\n                Y_ij = Ybus[i, j]\n\n                # S_ij = V_i * conj(I_ij) = V_i * conj(Y_ij * (V_i - V_j))\n                # Expand: S = (Vr + j*Vi) * conj(Y) * conj(Vr - j*Vi - Vr_j + j*Vi_j)\n                G = real(Y_ij)\n                B = imag(Y_ij)\n\n                if i == j:\n                    # Diagonal term\n                    Pi_calc += G * Vm_i_sq\n                    Qi_calc += -B * Vm_i_sq\n                else:\n                    # Off-diagonal term\n                    Pi_calc += Vr[i] * (G * Vr[j] + B * Vi[j]) + Vi[i] * (G * Vi[j] - B * Vr[j])\n                    Qi_calc += Vi[i] * (G * Vr[j] + B * Vi[j]) - Vr[i] * (G * Vi[j] - B * Vr[j])\n\n        # Power balance: generation - load = injection\n        g.append(Pg_net - Pd[i] - Pi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Qg_net - Qd[i] - Qi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Vm_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        h.append(Vm_sq)\n        lbh.append(Vm_min[i]**2)\n        ubh.append(Vm_max[i]**2)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        h.append(Pg[k])\n        lbh.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Pg_max[k])\n\n        h.append(Qg[k])\n        lbh.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branch_info:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        y = br['y']\n        yc_i = br['yc_i']\n        yc_j = br['yc_j']\n        T = br['T']\n        rate = br['rate']\n\n        if rate > 0:\n            # From side: S_ij = (y + yc_i)* * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2 - y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n            Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n\n            # Complex calculations for power flow\n            y_total_from = (y + yc_i) / abs(T)**2\n            G_from = real(y_total_from)\n            B_from = imag(y_total_from)\n\n            y_series = y / T\n            G_series = real(y_series)\n            B_series = imag(y_series)\n\n            Pij = G_from * Vm_i_sq - Vr[i] * (G_series * Vr[j] - B_series * Vi[j]) - Vi[i] * (G_series * Vi[j] + B_series * Vr[j])\n            Qij = -B_from * Vm_i_sq - Vi[i] * (G_series * Vr[j] - B_series * Vi[j]) + Vr[i] * (G_series * Vi[j] + B_series * Vr[j])\n\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n            # To side\n            Vm_j_sq = Vr[j]**2 + Vi[j]**2\n\n            y_total_to = y + yc_j\n            G_to = real(y_total_to)\n            B_to = imag(y_total_to)\n\n            y_series_rev = y / conj(T)\n            G_series_rev = real(y_series_rev)\n            B_series_rev = imag(y_series_rev)\n\n            Pji = G_to * Vm_j_sq - Vr[j] * (G_series_rev * Vr[i] - B_series_rev * Vi[i]) - Vi[j] * (G_series_rev * Vi[i] + B_series_rev * Vr[i])\n            Qji = -B_to * Vm_j_sq - Vi[j] * (G_series_rev * Vr[i] - B_series_rev * Vi[i]) + Vr[j] * (G_series_rev * Vi[i] + B_series_rev * Vr[i])\n\n            Sji_sq = Pji**2 + Qji**2\n            h.append(Sji_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n    # Stack all variables\n    x = vertcat(Vr, Vi, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # Build NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h)\n    }\n\n    # Bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage bounds (using magnitude limits already in constraints)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vr\n        ubx.append(2)\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vi\n        ubx.append(2)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        lbx.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Pg_max[k])\n        lbx.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    bounds = {\n        'lbx': lbx,\n        'ubx': ubx,\n        'lbg': lbg + lbh,\n        'ubg': ubg + ubh\n    }\n\n    # Initial guess (flat start with 1.0 p.u. voltage)\n    x0 = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(1.0)  # Vr\n        x0.append(0.0)  # Vi\n\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        x0.append((Pg_min[k] + Pg_max[k]) / 2)\n        x0.append(0.0)\n\n    return nlp, bounds, x0, {\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n        'branch_info': branch_info,\n        'Ybus': Ybus,\n        'Pd': Pd,\n        'Qd': Qd,\n        'bus_num_to_idx': bus_num_to_idx\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Building AC OPF model...\")\n    nlp, bounds, x0, info = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {info['n_bus']} buses, {info['n_gen']} generators, {info['n_branch']} branches\")\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-5,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.mehrotra_algorithm': 'yes'\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"Solving AC OPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=bounds['lbx'], ubx=bounds['ubx'],\n                 lbg=bounds['lbg'], ubg=bounds['ubg'])\n\n    return sol, info\n\ndef extract_solution(sol, info):\n    \"\"\"Extract and format the solution\"\"\"\n\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    n_bus = info['n_bus']\n    n_gen = info['n_gen']\n    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n\n    # Extract voltages\n    Vr = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Vi = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Vm = np.sqrt(Vr**2 + Vi**2)\n    Va = np.arctan2(Vi, Vr) * 180 / np.pi\n\n    # Extract generator dispatch\n    Pg = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for br in info['branch_info']:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        y = br['y']\n        yc_i = br['yc_i']\n        yc_j = br['yc_j']\n        T = br['T']\n\n        V_i = Vr[i] + 1j * Vi[i]\n        V_j = Vr[j] + 1j * Vi[j]\n\n        # From side flow\n        S_ij = (np.conj(y + yc_i) * abs(V_i)**2 / abs(T)**2 -\n                np.conj(y) * V_i * np.conj(V_j) / T)\n\n        # To side flow\n        S_ji = (np.conj(y + yc_j) * abs(V_j)**2 -\n                np.conj(y) * np.conj(V_i) * V_j / np.conj(T))\n\n        Sij_MVA = abs(S_ij) * baseMVA\n        Sji_MVA = abs(S_ji) * baseMVA\n        rate_MVA = br['rate'] * baseMVA\n\n        loading_pct = (max(Sij_MVA, Sji_MVA) / rate_MVA * 100) if rate_MVA > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': br['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': br['to_bus'],\n            'Sij_MVA': Sij_MVA,\n            'Sji_MVA': Sji_MVA,\n            'rate_MVA': rate_MVA,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm,\n        'Va': Va,\n        'Pg': Pg,\n        'Qg': Qg,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'objective': float(sol['f'])\n    }\n\ndef generate_report(sol, info, network):\n    \"\"\"Generate the JSON report\"\"\"\n\n    solution = extract_solution(sol, info)\n    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    Pg_MW = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA\n    Qg_MVAr = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA\n\n    total_gen_MW = float(np.sum(Pg_MW))\n    total_gen_MVAr = float(np.sum(Qg_MVAr))\n\n    total_load_MW = float(np.sum(info['Pd']) * baseMVA)\n    total_load_MVAr = float(np.sum(info['Qd']) * baseMVA)\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Generator data\n    generators = []\n    for k in range(info['n_gen']):\n        bus_idx = info['gen_bus_idx'][k]\n        bus_num = int(info['bus_data'][bus_idx, 0])\n        gen_data_row = info['gen_data'][k]\n\n        generators.append({\n            'id': k + 1,\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[k]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[k]),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen_data_row[9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen_data_row[8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen_data_row[4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen_data_row[3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus data\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(info['n_bus']):\n        bus_num = int(info['bus_data'][i, 0])\n        buses.append({\n            'id': bus_num,\n            'vm_pu': float(solution['Vm'][i]),\n            'va_deg': float(solution['Va'][i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(info['bus_data'][i, 12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(info['bus_data'][i, 11])\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches (top 10)\n    branch_flows = solution['branch_flows']\n    branch_flows_sorted = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n\n    most_loaded_branches = []\n    for br in branch_flows_sorted[:10]:\n        most_loaded_branches.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br['from_bus']),\n            'to_bus': int(br['to_bus']),\n            'loading_pct': float(br['loading_pct']),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(br['Sij_MVA']),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(br['Sji_MVA']),\n            'limit_MVA': float(br['rate_MVA'])\n        })\n\n    # Feasibility check (calculate violations)\n    # Power balance already satisfied by optimization\n    # Check voltage violations\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    for i in range(info['n_bus']):\n        Vm = solution['Vm'][i]\n        Vmin = info['bus_data'][i, 12]\n        Vmax = info['bus_data'][i, 11]\n        violation = max(0, Vmin - Vm, Vm - Vmax)\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n    for br in branch_flows:\n        if br['rate_MVA'] > 0:\n            overload = max(0, br['Sij_MVA'] - br['rate_MVA'], br['Sji_MVA'] - br['rate_MVA'])\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['objective']),\n            'total_load_MW': total_load_MW,\n            'total_load_MVAr': total_load_MVAr,\n            'total_generation_MW': total_gen_MW,\n            'total_generation_MVAr': total_gen_MVAr,\n            'total_losses_MW': total_losses_MW,\n            'solver_status': 'optimal'\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded_branches,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,  # From solver tolerance\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.001,\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function\"\"\"\n\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network_data('network.json')\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    sol, info = solve_acopf(network)\n\n    # Generate report\n    print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n    report = generate_report(sol, info, network)\n\n    # Save report\n    with open('report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"SOLUTION SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {report['summary']['total_generation_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {report['summary']['total_load_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Losses: {report['summary']['total_losses_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Solver Status: {report['summary']['solver_status']}\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"\\nReport saved to report.json\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n"}}
