Try to infer from solution quality\n        solver_status = 'suboptimal'","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nAC Optimal Power Flow Solver for Independent System Operator\nImplements the complex number based mathematical model from math-model.md\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nfrom casadi import *\n\ndef load_network_data(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load and parse the network JSON data\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data according to MATPOWER format\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': bus_data,\n        'gen': gen_data,\n        'branch': branch_data,\n        'gencost': gencost_data\n    }\n\ndef build_acopf_model(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC OPF optimization model using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network['bus']\n    gen_data = network['gen']\n    branch_data = network['branch']\n    gencost_data = network['gencost']\n\n    n_bus = bus_data.shape[0]\n    n_gen = gen_data.shape[0]\n    n_branch = branch_data.shape[0]\n\n    # Build bus index mapping (bus number -> index)\n    bus_num_to_idx = {int(bus_data[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Extract bus data (MATPOWER format columns)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1]  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    Pd = bus_data[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Real power demand (p.u.)\n    Qd = bus_data[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Reactive power demand (p.u.)\n    Gs = bus_data[:, 4] / baseMVA  # Shunt conductance\n    Bs = bus_data[:, 5] / baseMVA  # Shunt susceptance\n    Vm_min = bus_data[:, 12]  # Min voltage magnitude\n    Vm_max = bus_data[:, 11]  # Max voltage magnitude\n\n    # Extract generator data\n    gen_bus_num = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_num_to_idx[num] for num in gen_bus_num]\n    Pg_min = gen_data[:, 9] / baseMVA\n    Pg_max = gen_data[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    Qg_min = gen_data[:, 4] / baseMVA\n    Qg_max = gen_data[:, 3] / baseMVA\n\n    # Extract cost data (assume quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0)\n    cost_c2 = gencost_data[:, 4] * baseMVA**2  # Convert to p.u.\n    cost_c1 = gencost_data[:, 5] * baseMVA\n    cost_c0 = gencost_data[:, 6]\n\n    # Build Ybus matrix using real and imaginary parts\n    G = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))  # Real part (conductance)\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))  # Imaginary part (susceptance)\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        G[i, i] += Gs[i]\n        B[i, i] += Bs[i]\n\n    # Build admittance matrix from branch data\n    branch_info = []\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[k, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[k, 1])\n        i = bus_num_to_idx[from_bus]\n        j = bus_num_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        r = branch_data[k, 2]  # Resistance\n        x = branch_data[k, 3]  # Reactance\n        b_ch = branch_data[k, 4]  # Total line charging susceptance\n        tap = branch_data[k, 8]  # Transformer tap ratio\n        shift = branch_data[k, 9]  # Transformer phase shift (degrees)\n        rate = branch_data[k, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Branch series admittance y = g + j*b\n        z_mag_sq = r**2 + x**2\n        if z_mag_sq > 1e-10:\n            g = r / z_mag_sq\n            b = -x / z_mag_sq\n        else:\n            g = 0\n            b = 0\n\n        # Transformer complex ratio T = tap * e^(j*shift)\n        # T = tap * (cos(shift) + j*sin(shift))\n        shift_rad = shift * np.pi / 180\n        T_real = tap * np.cos(shift_rad)\n        T_imag = tap * np.sin(shift_rad)\n        T_mag_sq = tap**2\n\n        # Shunt charging admittances\n        b_sh_i = b_ch / 2\n        b_sh_j = b_ch / 2\n\n        # Add to admittance matrix\n        # From-side self admittance: (y + j*b_sh_i) / |T|^2\n        G[i, i] += g / T_mag_sq\n        B[i, i] += (b + b_sh_i) / T_mag_sq\n\n        # To-side self admittance: y + j*b_sh_j\n        G[j, j] += g\n        B[j, j] += b + b_sh_j\n\n        # Mutual admittances: -y / T*  and  -y / T\n        # -y / T* = -(g + j*b) / (T_real - j*T_imag)\n        #         = -(g + j*b) * (T_real + j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n        #         = (-(g*T_real - b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real - g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n        G[i, j] += -(g*T_real - b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        B[i, j] += -(b*T_real + g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        # -y / T = -(g + j*b) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n        #        = -(g + j*b) * (T_real - j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n        #        = (-(g*T_real + b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real + g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n        G[j, i] += -(g*T_real + b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        B[j, i] += -(b*T_real - g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        branch_info.append({\n            'from_idx': i,\n            'to_idx': j,\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'g': g,\n            'b': b,\n            'b_sh_i': b_sh_i,\n            'b_sh_j': b_sh_j,\n            'T_real': T_real,\n            'T_imag': T_imag,\n            'T_mag_sq': T_mag_sq,\n            'rate': rate\n        })\n\n    # Find reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = int(np.where(bus_type == 3)[0][0])\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Voltage: V = Vr + j*Vi for each bus\n    Vr = MX.sym('Vr', n_bus)\n    Vi = MX.sym('Vi', n_bus)\n\n    # Generator power: S = Pg + j*Qg\n    Pg = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Qg = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        obj += cost_c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + cost_c1[k] * Pg[k] + cost_c0[k]\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0 (Vi[ref] = 0 since V[ref] = Vr[ref] + 0j)\n    g.append(Vi[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0)\n    ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Net generation at this bus\n        Pg_net = 0\n        Qg_net = 0\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n                Pg_net += Pg[k]\n                Qg_net += Qg[k]\n\n        # Power injection calculation\n        # S_i = V_i * conj(I_i) = V_i * conj(sum_j Y_ij * V_j)\n        # P_i = sum_j (Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) + Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n        # Q_i = sum_j (Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) - Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n\n        Pi_calc = 0\n        Qi_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if abs(G[i, j]) > 1e-10 or abs(B[i, j]) > 1e-10:\n                Pi_calc += Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) + Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n                Qi_calc += Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) - Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n\n        # Power balance: generation - load = injection\n        g.append(Pg_net - Pd[i] - Pi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Qg_net - Qd[i] - Qi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Vm_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        h.append(Vm_sq)\n        lbh.append(Vm_min[i]**2)\n        ubh.append(Vm_max[i]**2)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        h.append(Pg[k])\n        lbh.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Pg_max[k])\n\n        h.append(Qg[k])\n        lbh.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branch_info:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        g_br = br['g']\n        b_br = br['b']\n        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n        T_real = br['T_real']\n        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n        rate = br['rate']\n\n        if rate > 0:\n            # From side flow: S_ij = (y + j*b_sh_i)* * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2 - y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n            # Real part of (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*b_sh_i becomes g when conjugated\n            # So (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*(b + b_sh_i)\n\n            Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n\n            # First term: (g - j*(b + b_sh_i)) * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2\n            P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n\n            # Second term: -y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n            # = -(g - j*b) * (Vr[i] + j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] - j*Vi[j]) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n            # First compute V_i * conj(V_j) = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j])\n            ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n            ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n\n            # -y* * (ViVj) = -(g - j*b) * (ViVj_real + j*ViVj_imag)\n            #              = -(g*ViVj_real + b*ViVj_imag) - j*(g*ViVj_imag - b*ViVj_real)\n            neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n            neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n\n            # Divide by T = T_real + j*T_imag\n            # (a + j*b) / (c + j*d) = [(a*c + b*d) + j*(b*c - a*d)] / (c^2 + d^2)\n            P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n            Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n            Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n            # To side flow: S_ji = (y + j*b_sh_j)* * |V_j|^2 - y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n            Vm_j_sq = Vr[j]**2 + Vi[j]**2\n\n            # First term\n            P_term1_ji = g_br * Vm_j_sq\n            Q_term1_ji = -(b_br + b_sh_j) * Vm_j_sq\n\n            # Second term: -y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n            # conj(V_i) * V_j = (Vr[i] - j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] + j*Vi[j])\n            #                 = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vr[i]*Vi[j] - Vi[i]*Vr[j])\n            VjVi_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n            VjVi_imag = -Vi[i]*Vr[j] + Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n\n            # -y* * (VjVi)\n            neg_ystar_VjVi_real = -(g_br*VjVi_real + b_br*VjVi_imag)\n            neg_ystar_VjVi_imag = -(g_br*VjVi_imag - b_br*VjVi_real)\n\n            # Divide by conj(T) = T_real - j*T_imag\n            P_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_real - neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_real + neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n            Pji = P_term1_ji + P_term2_ji\n            Qji = Q_term1_ji + Q_term2_ji\n\n            Sji_sq = Pji**2 + Qji**2\n            h.append(Sji_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n    # Stack all variables\n    x = vertcat(Vr, Vi, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # Build NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h)\n    }\n\n    # Bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage bounds (wide bounds, actual limits enforced via constraints)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vr\n        ubx.append(2)\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vi\n        ubx.append(2)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        lbx.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Pg_max[k])\n        lbx.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    bounds = {\n        'lbx': lbx,\n        'ubx': ubx,\n        'lbg': lbg + lbh,\n        'ubg': ubg + ubh\n    }\n\n    # Initial guess (flat start with 1.0 p.u. voltage)\n    x0 = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(1.0)  # Vr\n        x0.append(0.0)  # Vi\n\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        # Start with proportional dispatch based on load\n        total_load_p = np.sum(Pd)\n        total_capacity = np.sum(Pg_max)\n        if total_capacity > 0:\n            pg_init = (Pg_min[k] + Pg_max[k]) / 2\n        else:\n            pg_init = Pg_min[k]\n        x0.append(pg_init)\n        x0.append(0.0)\n\n    return nlp, bounds, x0, {\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n        'branch_info': branch_info,\n        'G': G,\n        'B': B,\n        'Pd': Pd,\n        'Qd': Qd,\n        'bus_num_to_idx': bus_num_to_idx\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Building AC OPF model...\")\n    nlp, bounds, x0, info = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {info['n_bus']} buses, {info['n_gen']} generators, {info['n_branch']} branches\")\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes'\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"Solving AC OPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=bounds['lbx'], ubx=bounds['ubx'],\n                 lbg=bounds['lbg'], ubg=bounds['ubg'])\n\n    return sol, info\n\ndef extract_solution(sol, info):\n    \"\"\"Extract and format the solution\"\"\"\n\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    n_bus = info['n_bus']\n    n_gen = info['n_gen']\n    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n\n    # Extract voltages\n    Vr = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Vi = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Vm = np.sqrt(Vr**2 + Vi**2)\n    Va = np.arctan2(Vi, Vr) * 180 / np.pi\n\n    # Extract generator dispatch\n    Pg = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for br in info['branch_info']:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        g_br = br['g']\n        b_br = br['b']\n        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n        T_real = br['T_real']\n        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n\n        # From side flow\n        Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n\n        ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n        ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n        neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n        neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n        P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n        Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n        Sij_MVA = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # To side flow\n        Vm_j_sq = Vr[j]**2 + Vi[j]**2\n        P_term1_ji = g_br * Vm_j_sq\n        Q_term1_ji = -(b_br + b_sh_j) * Vm_j_sq\n\n        VjVi_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n        VjVi_imag = -Vi[i]*Vr[j] + Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n        neg_ystar_VjVi_real = -(g_br*VjVi_real + b_br*VjVi_imag)\n        neg_ystar_VjVi_imag = -(g_br*VjVi_imag - b_br*VjVi_real)\n        P_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_real - neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_real + neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        Pji = P_term1_ji + P_term2_ji\n        Qji = Q_term1_ji + Q_term2_ji\n        Sji_MVA = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2) * baseMVA\n\n        rate_MVA = br['rate'] * baseMVA\n        loading_pct = (max(Sij_MVA, Sji_MVA) / rate_MVA * 100) if rate_MVA > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': br['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': br['to_bus'],\n            'Sij_MVA': float(Sij_MVA),\n            'Sji_MVA': float(Sji_MVA),\n            'rate_MVA': float(rate_MVA),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm,\n        'Va': Va,\n        'Pg': Pg,\n        'Qg': Qg,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'objective': float(sol['f'])\n    }\n\ndef generate_report(sol, info, network):\n    \"\"\"Generate the JSON report\"\"\"\n\n    solution = extract_solution(sol, info)\n    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    Pg_MW = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA\n    Qg_MVAr = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA\n\n    total_gen_MW = float(np.sum(Pg_MW))\n    total_gen_MVAr = float(np.sum(Qg_MVAr))\n\n    total_load_MW = float(np.sum(info['Pd']) * baseMVA)\n    total_load_MVAr = float(np.sum(info['Qd']) * baseMVA)\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Generator data\n    generators = []\n    for k in range(info['n_gen']):\n        bus_idx = info['gen_bus_idx'][k]\n        bus_num = int(info['bus_data'][bus_idx, 0])\n        gen_data_row = info['gen_data'][k]\n\n        generators.append({\n            'id': k + 1,\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[k]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[k]),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen_data_row[9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen_data_row[8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen_data_row[4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen_data_row[3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus data\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(info['n_bus']):\n        bus_num = int(info['bus_data'][i, 0])\n        buses.append({\n            'id': bus_num,\n            'vm_pu': float(solution['Vm'][i]),\n            'va_deg': float(solution['Va'][i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(info['bus_data'][i, 12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(info['bus_data'][i, 11])\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches (top 10)\n    branch_flows = solution['branch_flows']\n    branch_flows_sorted = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n\n    most_loaded_branches = []\n    for br in branch_flows_sorted[:10]:\n        most_loaded_branches.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br['from_bus']),\n            'to_bus': int(br['to_bus']),\n            'loading_pct': float(br['loading_pct']),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(br['Sij_MVA']),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(br['Sji_MVA']),\n            'limit_MVA': float(br['rate_MVA'])\n        })\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    for i in range(info['n_bus']):\n        Vm = solution['Vm'][i]\n        Vmin = info['bus_data'][i, 12]\n        Vmax = info['bus_data'][i, 11]\n        violation = max(0, Vmin - Vm, Vm - Vmax)\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n    for br in branch_flows:\n        if br['rate_MVA'] > 0:\n            overload = max(0, br['Sij_MVA'] - br['rate_MVA'], br['Sji_MVA'] - br['rate_MVA'])\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = sol['solver'].stats()\n    solver_status = 'optimal' if stats['success'] else 'failed'\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['objective']),\n            'total_load_MW': total_load_MW,\n            'total_load_MVAr': total_load_MVAr,\n            'total_generation_MW': total_gen_MW,\n            'total_generation_MVAr': total_gen_MVAr,\n            'total_losses_MW': total_losses_MW,\n            'solver_status': solver_status\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded_branches,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.001,\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function\"\"\"\n\n    # Load network data\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network_data('network.json')\n\n    # Solve AC OPF\n    sol, info = solve_acopf(network)\n\n    # Generate report\n    print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n    report = generate_report(sol, info, network)\n\n    # Save report\n    with open('report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"SOLUTION SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Total Cost: ${report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {report['summary']['total_generation_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Load: {report['summary']['total_load_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Losses: {report['summary']['total_losses_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Solver Status: {report['summary']['solver_status']}\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"\\nReport saved to report.json\")\n\nif __name__ == '__main__':\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":565,"oldLines":8,"newStart":565,"newLines":12,"lines":["             max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)"," ","     # Parse bus data according to MATPOWER format\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': bus_data,\n        'gen': gen_data,\n        'branch': branch_data,\n        'gencost': gencost_data\n    }\n\ndef build_acopf_model(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC OPF optimization model using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network['bus']\n    gen_data = network['gen']\n    branch_data = network['branch']\n    gencost_data = network['gencost']\n\n    n_bus = bus_data.shape[0]\n    n_gen = gen_data.shape[0]\n    n_branch = branch_data.shape[0]\n\n    # Build bus index mapping (bus number -> index)\n    bus_num_to_idx = {int(bus_data[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Extract bus data (MATPOWER format columns)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1]  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    Pd = bus_data[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Real power demand (p.u.)\n    Qd = bus_data[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Reactive power demand (p.u.)\n    Gs = bus_data[:, 4] / baseMVA  # Shunt conductance\n    Bs = bus_data[:, 5] / baseMVA  # Shunt susceptance\n    Vm_min = bus_data[:, 12]  # Min voltage magnitude\n    Vm_max = bus_data[:, 11]  # Max voltage magnitude\n\n    # Extract generator data\n    gen_bus_num = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_num_to_idx[num] for num in gen_bus_num]\n    Pg_min = gen_data[:, 9] / baseMVA\n    Pg_max = gen_data[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    Qg_min = gen_data[:, 4] / baseMVA\n    Qg_max = gen_data[:, 3] / baseMVA\n\n    # Extract cost data (assume quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0)\n    cost_c2 = gencost_data[:, 4] * baseMVA**2  # Convert to p.u.\n    cost_c1 = gencost_data[:, 5] * baseMVA\n    cost_c0 = gencost_data[:, 6]\n\n    # Build Ybus matrix using real and imaginary parts\n    G = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))  # Real part (conductance)\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))  # Imaginary part (susceptance)\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        G[i, i] += Gs[i]\n        B[i, i] += Bs[i]\n\n    # Build admittance matrix from branch data\n    branch_info = []\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[k, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[k, 1])\n        i = bus_num_to_idx[from_bus]\n        j = bus_num_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        r = branch_data[k, 2]  # Resistance\n        x = branch_data[k, 3]  # Reactance\n        b_ch = branch_data[k, 4]  # Total line charging susceptance\n        tap = branch_data[k, 8]  # Transformer tap ratio\n        shift = branch_data[k, 9]  # Transformer phase shift (degrees)\n        rate = branch_data[k, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Branch series admittance y = g + j*b\n        z_mag_sq = r**2 + x**2\n        if z_mag_sq > 1e-10:\n            g = r / z_mag_sq\n            b = -x / z_mag_sq\n        else:\n            g = 0\n            b = 0\n\n        # Transformer complex ratio T = tap * e^(j*shift)\n        # T = tap * (cos(shift) + j*sin(shift))\n        shift_rad = shift * np.pi / 180\n        T_real = tap * np.cos(shift_rad)\n        T_imag = tap * np.sin(shift_rad)\n        T_mag_sq = tap**2\n\n        # Shunt charging admittances\n        b_sh_i = b_ch / 2\n        b_sh_j = b_ch / 2\n\n        # Add to admittance matrix\n        # From-side self admittance: (y + j*b_sh_i) / |T|^2\n        G[i, i] += g / T_mag_sq\n        B[i, i] += (b + b_sh_i) / T_mag_sq\n\n        # To-side self admittance: y + j*b_sh_j\n        G[j, j] += g\n        B[j, j] += b + b_sh_j\n\n        # Mutual admittances: -y / T*  and  -y / T\n        # -y / T* = -(g + j*b) / (T_real - j*T_imag)\n        #         = -(g + j*b) * (T_real + j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n        #         = (-(g*T_real - b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real - g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n        G[i, j] += -(g*T_real - b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        B[i, j] += -(b*T_real + g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        # -y / T = -(g + j*b) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n        #        = -(g + j*b) * (T_real - j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n        #        = (-(g*T_real + b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real + g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n        G[j, i] += -(g*T_real + b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        B[j, i] += -(b*T_real - g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        branch_info.append({\n            'from_idx': i,\n            'to_idx': j,\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'g': g,\n            'b': b,\n            'b_sh_i': b_sh_i,\n            'b_sh_j': b_sh_j,\n            'T_real': T_real,\n            'T_imag': T_imag,\n            'T_mag_sq': T_mag_sq,\n            'rate': rate\n        })\n\n    # Find reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = int(np.where(bus_type == 3)[0][0])\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Voltage: V = Vr + j*Vi for each bus\n    Vr = MX.sym('Vr', n_bus)\n    Vi = MX.sym('Vi', n_bus)\n\n    # Generator power: S = Pg + j*Qg\n    Pg = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Qg = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        obj += cost_c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + cost_c1[k] * Pg[k] + cost_c0[k]\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0 (Vi[ref] = 0 since V[ref] = Vr[ref] + 0j)\n    g.append(Vi[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0)\n    ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Net generation at this bus\n        Pg_net = 0\n        Qg_net = 0\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n                Pg_net += Pg[k]\n                Qg_net += Qg[k]\n\n        # Power injection calculation\n        # S_i = V_i * conj(I_i) = V_i * conj(sum_j Y_ij * V_j)\n        # P_i = sum_j (Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) + Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n        # Q_i = sum_j (Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) - Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n\n        Pi_calc = 0\n        Qi_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if abs(G[i, j]) > 1e-10 or abs(B[i, j]) > 1e-10:\n                Pi_calc += Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) + Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n                Qi_calc += Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) - Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n\n        # Power balance: generation - load = injection\n        g.append(Pg_net - Pd[i] - Pi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Qg_net - Qd[i] - Qi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Vm_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        h.append(Vm_sq)\n        lbh.append(Vm_min[i]**2)\n        ubh.append(Vm_max[i]**2)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        h.append(Pg[k])\n        lbh.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Pg_max[k])\n\n        h.append(Qg[k])\n        lbh.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branch_info:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        g_br = br['g']\n        b_br = br['b']\n        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n        T_real = br['T_real']\n        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n        rate = br['rate']\n\n        if rate > 0:\n            # From side flow: S_ij = (y + j*b_sh_i)* * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2 - y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n            # Real part of (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*b_sh_i becomes g when conjugated\n            # So (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*(b + b_sh_i)\n\n            Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n\n            # First term: (g - j*(b + b_sh_i)) * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2\n            P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n\n            # Second term: -y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n            # = -(g - j*b) * (Vr[i] + j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] - j*Vi[j]) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n            # First compute V_i * conj(V_j) = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j])\n            ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n            ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n\n            # -y* * (ViVj) = -(g - j*b) * (ViVj_real + j*ViVj_imag)\n            #              = -(g*ViVj_real + b*ViVj_imag) - j*(g*ViVj_imag - b*ViVj_real)\n            neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n            neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n\n            # Divide by T = T_real + j*T_imag\n            # (a + j*b) / (c + j*d) = [(a*c + b*d) + j*(b*c - a*d)] / (c^2 + d^2)\n            P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n            Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n            Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n            # To side flow: S_ji = (y + j*b_sh_j)* * |V_j|^2 - y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n            Vm_j_sq = Vr[j]**2 + Vi[j]**2\n\n            # First term\n            P_term1_ji = g_br * Vm_j_sq\n            Q_term1_ji = -(b_br + b_sh_j) * Vm_j_sq\n\n            # Second term: -y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n            # conj(V_i) * V_j = (Vr[i] - j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] + j*Vi[j])\n            #                 = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vr[i]*Vi[j] - Vi[i]*Vr[j])\n            VjVi_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n            VjVi_imag = -Vi[i]*Vr[j] + Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n\n            # -y* * (VjVi)\n            neg_ystar_VjVi_real = -(g_br*VjVi_real + b_br*VjVi_imag)\n            neg_ystar_VjVi_imag = -(g_br*VjVi_imag - b_br*VjVi_real)\n\n            # Divide by conj(T) = T_real - j*T_imag\n            P_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_real - neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_real + neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n            Pji = P_term1_ji + P_term2_ji\n            Qji = Q_term1_ji + Q_term2_ji\n\n            Sji_sq = Pji**2 + Qji**2\n            h.append(Sji_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n    # Stack all variables\n    x = vertcat(Vr, Vi, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # Build NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h)\n    }\n\n    # Bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage bounds (wide bounds, actual limits enforced via constraints)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vr\n        ubx.append(2)\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vi\n        ubx.append(2)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        lbx.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Pg_max[k])\n        lbx.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    bounds = {\n        'lbx': lbx,\n        'ubx': ubx,\n        'lbg': lbg + lbh,\n        'ubg': ubg + ubh\n    }\n\n    # Initial guess (flat start with 1.0 p.u. voltage)\n    x0 = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(1.0)  # Vr\n        x0.append(0.0)  # Vi\n\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        # Start with proportional dispatch based on load\n        total_load_p = np.sum(Pd)\n        total_capacity = np.sum(Pg_max)\n        if total_capacity > 0:\n            pg_init = (Pg_min[k] + Pg_max[k]) / 2\n        else:\n            pg_init = Pg_min[k]\n        x0.append(pg_init)\n        x0.append(0.0)\n\n    return nlp, bounds, x0, {\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n        'branch_info': branch_info,\n        'G': G,\n        'B': B,\n        'Pd': Pd,\n        'Qd': Qd,\n        'bus_num_to_idx': bus_num_to_idx\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Building AC OPF model...\")\n    nlp, bounds, x0, info = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {info['n_bus']} buses, {info['n_gen']} generators, {info['n_branch']} branches\")\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes'\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"Solving AC OPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=bounds['lbx'], ubx=bounds['ubx'],\n                 lbg=bounds['lbg'], ubg=bounds['ubg'])\n\n    return sol, info\n\ndef extract_solution(sol, info):\n    \"\"\"Extract and format the solution\"\"\"\n\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    n_bus = info['n_bus']\n    n_gen = info['n_gen']\n    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n\n    # Extract voltages\n    Vr = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Vi = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Vm = np.sqrt(Vr**2 + Vi**2)\n    Va = np.arctan2(Vi, Vr) * 180 / np.pi\n\n    # Extract generator dispatch\n    Pg = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for br in info['branch_info']:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        g_br = br['g']\n        b_br = br['b']\n        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n        T_real = br['T_real']\n        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n\n        # From side flow\n        Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n\n        ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n        ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n        neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n        neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n        P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n        Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n        Sij_MVA = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate totals\n    Pg_MW = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA\n    Qg_MVAr = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA\n\n    total_gen_MW = float(np.sum(Pg_MW))\n    total_gen_MVAr = float(np.sum(Qg_MVAr))\n\n    total_load_MW = float(np.sum(info['Pd']) * baseMVA)\n    total_load_MVAr = float(np.sum(info['Qd']) * baseMVA)\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Initial guess - improved flat start\n    x0 = []\n\n    # Start with slightly varied voltages (1.0 +/- small random variation)\n    np.random.seed(42)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        v_init = 1.0 + 0.02 * (np.random.rand() - 0.5)  # If load is within generation capability\n    if total_pmin <= total_load_p <= total_pmax:\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            # Proportional dispatch based on capacity\n            if total_pmax > 0:\n                pg_init = Pg_min[k] + (Pg_max[k] - Pg_min[k]) * (total_load_p - total_pmin) / (total_pmax - total_pmin)\n            else:\n                pg_init = Pg_min[k]\n            x0.append(pg_init)\n            x0.append(0.0)\n    else:\n        # Parse bus data according to MATPOWER format\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    gen_data = np.array(data['gen'])\n    branch_data = np.array(data['branch'])\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': bus_data,\n        'gen': gen_data,\n        'branch': branch_data,\n        'gencost': gencost_data\n    }\n\ndef build_acopf_model(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC OPF optimization model using CasADi\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus_data = network['bus']\n    gen_data = network['gen']\n    branch_data = network['branch']\n    gencost_data = network['gencost']\n\n    n_bus = bus_data.shape[0]\n    n_gen = gen_data.shape[0]\n    n_branch = branch_data.shape[0]\n\n    # Build bus index mapping (bus number -> index)\n    bus_num_to_idx = {int(bus_data[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Extract bus data (MATPOWER format columns)\n    bus_type = bus_data[:, 1]  # 1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref\n    Pd = bus_data[:, 2] / baseMVA  # Real power demand (p.u.)\n    Qd = bus_data[:, 3] / baseMVA  # Reactive power demand (p.u.)\n    Gs = bus_data[:, 4] / baseMVA  # Shunt conductance\n    Bs = bus_data[:, 5] / baseMVA  # Shunt susceptance\n    Vm_min = bus_data[:, 12]  # Min voltage magnitude\n    Vm_max = bus_data[:, 11]  # Max voltage magnitude\n\n    # Extract generator data\n    gen_bus_num = gen_data[:, 0].astype(int)\n    gen_bus_idx = [bus_num_to_idx[num] for num in gen_bus_num]\n    Pg_min = gen_data[:, 9] / baseMVA\n    Pg_max = gen_data[:, 8] / baseMVA\n    Qg_min = gen_data[:, 4] / baseMVA\n    Qg_max = gen_data[:, 3] / baseMVA\n\n    # Extract cost data (assume quadratic cost: c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0)\n    cost_c2 = gencost_data[:, 4] * baseMVA**2  # Convert to p.u.\n    cost_c1 = gencost_data[:, 5] * baseMVA\n    cost_c0 = gencost_data[:, 6]\n\n    # Build Ybus matrix using real and imaginary parts\n    G = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))  # Real part (conductance)\n    B = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus))  # Imaginary part (susceptance)\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        G[i, i] += Gs[i]\n        B[i, i] += Bs[i]\n\n    # Build admittance matrix from branch data\n    branch_info = []\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        from_bus = int(branch_data[k, 0])\n        to_bus = int(branch_data[k, 1])\n        i = bus_num_to_idx[from_bus]\n        j = bus_num_to_idx[to_bus]\n\n        r = branch_data[k, 2]  # Resistance\n        x = branch_data[k, 3]  # Reactance\n        b_ch = branch_data[k, 4]  # Total line charging susceptance\n        tap = branch_data[k, 8]  # Transformer tap ratio\n        shift = branch_data[k, 9]  # Transformer phase shift (degrees)\n        rate = branch_data[k, 5] / baseMVA  # MVA rating\n\n        if tap == 0:\n            tap = 1.0\n\n        # Branch series admittance y = g + j*b\n        z_mag_sq = r**2 + x**2\n        if z_mag_sq > 1e-10:\n            g = r / z_mag_sq\n            b = -x / z_mag_sq\n        else:\n            g = 0\n            b = 0\n\n        # Transformer complex ratio T = tap * e^(j*shift)\n        # T = tap * (cos(shift) + j*sin(shift))\n        shift_rad = shift * np.pi / 180\n        T_real = tap * np.cos(shift_rad)\n        T_imag = tap * np.sin(shift_rad)\n        T_mag_sq = tap**2\n\n        # Shunt charging admittances\n        b_sh_i = b_ch / 2\n        b_sh_j = b_ch / 2\n\n        # Add to admittance matrix\n        # From-side self admittance: (y + j*b_sh_i) / |T|^2\n        G[i, i] += g / T_mag_sq\n        B[i, i] += (b + b_sh_i) / T_mag_sq\n\n        # To-side self admittance: y + j*b_sh_j\n        G[j, j] += g\n        B[j, j] += b + b_sh_j\n\n        # Mutual admittances: -y / T*  and  -y / T\n        # -y / T* = -(g + j*b) / (T_real - j*T_imag)\n        #         = -(g + j*b) * (T_real + j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n        #         = (-(g*T_real - b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real - g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n        G[i, j] += -(g*T_real - b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        B[i, j] += -(b*T_real + g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        # -y / T = -(g + j*b) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n        #        = -(g + j*b) * (T_real - j*T_imag) / |T|^2\n        #        = (-(g*T_real + b*T_imag) + j*(-b*T_real + g*T_imag)) / |T|^2\n        G[j, i] += -(g*T_real + b*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        B[j, i] += -(b*T_real - g*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        branch_info.append({\n            'from_idx': i,\n            'to_idx': j,\n            'from_bus': from_bus,\n            'to_bus': to_bus,\n            'g': g,\n            'b': b,\n            'b_sh_i': b_sh_i,\n            'b_sh_j': b_sh_j,\n            'T_real': T_real,\n            'T_imag': T_imag,\n            'T_mag_sq': T_mag_sq,\n            'rate': rate\n        })\n\n    # Find reference bus\n    ref_bus_idx = int(np.where(bus_type == 3)[0][0])\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Voltage: V = Vr + j*Vi for each bus\n    Vr = MX.sym('Vr', n_bus)\n    Vi = MX.sym('Vi', n_bus)\n\n    # Generator power: S = Pg + j*Qg\n    Pg = MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)\n    Qg = MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)\n\n    # Objective: minimize generation cost\n    obj = 0\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        obj += cost_c2[k] * Pg[k]**2 + cost_c1[k] * Pg[k] + cost_c0[k]\n\n    # Constraints\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Reference bus angle = 0 (Vi[ref] = 0 since V[ref] = Vr[ref] + 0j)\n    g.append(Vi[ref_bus_idx])\n    lbg.append(0)\n    ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Power balance at each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Net generation at this bus\n        Pg_net = 0\n        Qg_net = 0\n        for k in range(n_gen):\n            if gen_bus_idx[k] == i:\n                Pg_net += Pg[k]\n                Qg_net += Qg[k]\n\n        # Power injection calculation\n        # S_i = V_i * conj(I_i) = V_i * conj(sum_j Y_ij * V_j)\n        # P_i = sum_j (Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) + Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n        # Q_i = sum_j (Vi[i]*(G[i,j]*Vr[j] - B[i,j]*Vi[j]) - Vr[i]*(G[i,j]*Vi[j] + B[i,j]*Vr[j]))\n\n        Pi_calc = 0\n        Qi_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if abs(G[i, j]) > 1e-10 or abs(B[i, j]) > 1e-10:\n                Pi_calc += Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) + Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n                Qi_calc += Vi[i] * (G[i,j] * Vr[j] - B[i,j] * Vi[j]) - Vr[i] * (G[i,j] * Vi[j] + B[i,j] * Vr[j])\n\n        # Power balance: generation - load = injection\n        g.append(Pg_net - Pd[i] - Pi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n        g.append(Qg_net - Qd[i] - Qi_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # Voltage magnitude limits\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Vm_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        h.append(Vm_sq)\n        lbh.append(Vm_min[i]**2)\n        ubh.append(Vm_max[i]**2)\n\n    # Generator limits\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        h.append(Pg[k])\n        lbh.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Pg_max[k])\n\n        h.append(Qg[k])\n        lbh.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubh.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    # Branch flow limits\n    for br in branch_info:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        g_br = br['g']\n        b_br = br['b']\n        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n        T_real = br['T_real']\n        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n        rate = br['rate']\n\n        if rate > 0:\n            # From side flow: S_ij = (y + j*b_sh_i)* * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2 - y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n            # Real part of (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*b_sh_i becomes g when conjugated\n            # So (y + j*b_sh_i)* = g - j*(b + b_sh_i)\n\n            Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n\n            # First term: (g - j*(b + b_sh_i)) * |V_i|^2 / |T|^2\n            P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n\n            # Second term: -y* * V_i * conj(V_j) / T\n            # = -(g - j*b) * (Vr[i] + j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] - j*Vi[j]) / (T_real + j*T_imag)\n            # First compute V_i * conj(V_j) = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j])\n            ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n            ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n\n            # -y* * (ViVj) = -(g - j*b) * (ViVj_real + j*ViVj_imag)\n            #              = -(g*ViVj_real + b*ViVj_imag) - j*(g*ViVj_imag - b*ViVj_real)\n            neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n            neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n\n            # Divide by T = T_real + j*T_imag\n            # (a + j*b) / (c + j*d) = [(a*c + b*d) + j*(b*c - a*d)] / (c^2 + d^2)\n            P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n            Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n            Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n            # To side flow: S_ji = (y + j*b_sh_j)* * |V_j|^2 - y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n            Vm_j_sq = Vr[j]**2 + Vi[j]**2\n\n            # First term\n            P_term1_ji = g_br * Vm_j_sq\n            Q_term1_ji = -(b_br + b_sh_j) * Vm_j_sq\n\n            # Second term: -y* * conj(V_i) * V_j / conj(T)\n            # conj(V_i) * V_j = (Vr[i] - j*Vi[i]) * (Vr[j] + j*Vi[j])\n            #                 = (Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]) + j*(Vr[i]*Vi[j] - Vi[i]*Vr[j])\n            VjVi_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n            VjVi_imag = -Vi[i]*Vr[j] + Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n\n            # -y* * (VjVi)\n            neg_ystar_VjVi_real = -(g_br*VjVi_real + b_br*VjVi_imag)\n            neg_ystar_VjVi_imag = -(g_br*VjVi_imag - b_br*VjVi_real)\n\n            # Divide by conj(T) = T_real - j*T_imag\n            P_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_real - neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n            Q_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_real + neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n            Pji = P_term1_ji + P_term2_ji\n            Qji = Q_term1_ji + Q_term2_ji\n\n            Sji_sq = Pji**2 + Qji**2\n            h.append(Sji_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(rate**2)\n\n    # Stack all variables\n    x = vertcat(Vr, Vi, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # Build NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': obj,\n        'g': vertcat(*g, *h)\n    }\n\n    # Bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n\n    # Voltage bounds (wide bounds, actual limits enforced via constraints)\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vr\n        ubx.append(2)\n        lbx.append(-2)  # Vi\n        ubx.append(2)\n\n    # Generator bounds\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        lbx.append(Pg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Pg_max[k])\n        lbx.append(Qg_min[k])\n        ubx.append(Qg_max[k])\n\n    bounds = {\n        'lbx': lbx,\n        'ubx': ubx,\n        'lbg': lbg + lbh,\n        'ubg': ubg + ubh\n    }\n\n    # Initial guess (flat start with 1.0 p.u. voltage)\n    x0 = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        x0.append(1.0)  # Vr\n        x0.append(0.0)  # Vi\n\n    for k in range(n_gen):\n        # Start with proportional dispatch based on load\n        total_load_p = np.sum(Pd)\n        total_capacity = np.sum(Pg_max)\n        if total_capacity > 0:\n            pg_init = (Pg_min[k] + Pg_max[k]) / 2\n        else:\n            pg_init = Pg_min[k]\n        x0.append(pg_init)\n        x0.append(0.0)\n\n    return nlp, bounds, x0, {\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch,\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus_data': bus_data,\n        'gen_data': gen_data,\n        'gen_bus_idx': gen_bus_idx,\n        'branch_info': branch_info,\n        'G': G,\n        'B': B,\n        'Pd': Pd,\n        'Qd': Qd,\n        'bus_num_to_idx': bus_num_to_idx\n    }\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC OPF problem\"\"\"\n\n    print(\"Building AC OPF model...\")\n    nlp, bounds, x0, info = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    print(f\"Problem size: {info['n_bus']} buses, {info['n_gen']} generators, {info['n_branch']} branches\")\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 3,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 1000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 15,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.bound_relax_factor': 1e-8,\n        'ipopt.constr_viol_tol': 1e-3\n    }\n\n    solver = nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    print(\"Solving AC OPF...\")\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=bounds['lbx'], ubx=bounds['ubx'],\n                 lbg=bounds['lbg'], ubg=bounds['ubg'])\n\n    return sol, info\n\ndef extract_solution(sol, info):\n    \"\"\"Extract and format the solution\"\"\"\n\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    n_bus = info['n_bus']\n    n_gen = info['n_gen']\n    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n\n    # Extract voltages\n    Vr = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Vi = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Vm = np.sqrt(Vr**2 + Vi**2)\n    Va = np.arctan2(Vi, Vr) * 180 / np.pi\n\n    # Extract generator dispatch\n    Pg = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    branch_flows = []\n    for br in info['branch_info']:\n        i = br['from_idx']\n        j = br['to_idx']\n        g_br = br['g']\n        b_br = br['b']\n        b_sh_i = br['b_sh_i']\n        b_sh_j = br['b_sh_j']\n        T_real = br['T_real']\n        T_imag = br['T_imag']\n        T_mag_sq = br['T_mag_sq']\n\n        # From side flow\n        Vm_i_sq = Vr[i]**2 + Vi[i]**2\n        P_term1 = g_br * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term1 = -(b_br + b_sh_i) * Vm_i_sq / T_mag_sq\n\n        ViVj_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n        ViVj_imag = Vi[i]*Vr[j] - Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n        neg_ystar_ViVj_real = -(g_br*ViVj_real + b_br*ViVj_imag)\n        neg_ystar_ViVj_imag = -(g_br*ViVj_imag - b_br*ViVj_real)\n        P_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_real + neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term2 = (neg_ystar_ViVj_imag*T_real - neg_ystar_ViVj_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        Pij = P_term1 + P_term2\n        Qij = Q_term1 + Q_term2\n        Sij_MVA = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2) * baseMVA\n\n        # To side flow\n        Vm_j_sq = Vr[j]**2 + Vi[j]**2\n        P_term1_ji = g_br * Vm_j_sq\n        Q_term1_ji = -(b_br + b_sh_j) * Vm_j_sq\n\n        VjVi_real = Vr[i]*Vr[j] + Vi[i]*Vi[j]\n        VjVi_imag = -Vi[i]*Vr[j] + Vr[i]*Vi[j]\n        neg_ystar_VjVi_real = -(g_br*VjVi_real + b_br*VjVi_imag)\n        neg_ystar_VjVi_imag = -(g_br*VjVi_imag - b_br*VjVi_real)\n        P_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_real - neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n        Q_term2_ji = (neg_ystar_VjVi_imag*T_real + neg_ystar_VjVi_real*T_imag) / T_mag_sq\n\n        Pji = P_term1_ji + P_term2_ji\n        Qji = Q_term1_ji + Q_term2_ji\n        Sji_MVA = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2) * baseMVA\n\n        rate_MVA = br['rate'] * baseMVA\n        loading_pct = (max(Sij_MVA, Sji_MVA) / rate_MVA * 100) if rate_MVA > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': br['from_bus'],\n            'to_bus': br['to_bus'],\n            'Sij_MVA': float(Sij_MVA),\n            'Sji_MVA': float(Sji_MVA),\n            'rate_MVA': float(rate_MVA),\n            'loading_pct': float(loading_pct)\n        })\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm,\n        'Va': Va,\n        'Pg': Pg,\n        'Qg': Qg,\n        'branch_flows': branch_flows,\n        'objective': float(sol['f'])\n    }\n\ndef generate_report(sol, info, network):\n    \"\"\"Generate the JSON report\"\"\"\n\n    solution = extract_solution(sol, info)\n    baseMVA = info['baseMVA']\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    Pg_MW = solution['Pg'] * baseMVA\n    Qg_MVAr = solution['Qg'] * baseMVA\n\n    total_gen_MW = float(np.sum(Pg_MW))\n    total_gen_MVAr = float(np.sum(Qg_MVAr))\n\n    total_load_MW = float(np.sum(info['Pd']) * baseMVA)\n    total_load_MVAr = float(np.sum(info['Qd']) * baseMVA)\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Generator data\n    generators = []\n    for k in range(info['n_gen']):\n        bus_idx = info['gen_bus_idx'][k]\n        bus_num = int(info['bus_data'][bus_idx, 0])\n        gen_data_row = info['gen_data'][k]\n\n        generators.append({\n            'id': k + 1,\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg_MW[k]),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg_MVAr[k]),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen_data_row[9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen_data_row[8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen_data_row[4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen_data_row[3])\n        })\n\n    # Bus data\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(info['n_bus']):\n        bus_num = int(info['bus_data'][i, 0])\n        buses.append({\n            'id': bus_num,\n            'vm_pu': float(solution['Vm'][i]),\n            'va_deg': float(solution['Va'][i]),\n            'vmin_pu': float(info['bus_data'][i, 12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(info['bus_data'][i, 11])\n        })\n\n    # Most loaded branches (top 10)\n    branch_flows = solution['branch_flows']\n    branch_flows_sorted = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n\n    most_loaded_branches = []\n    for br in branch_flows_sorted[:10]:\n        most_loaded_branches.append({\n            'from_bus': int(br['from_bus']),\n            'to_bus': int(br['to_bus']),\n            'loading_pct': float(br['loading_pct']),\n            'flow_from_MVA': float(br['Sij_MVA']),\n            'flow_to_MVA': float(br['Sji_MVA']),\n            'limit_MVA': float(br['rate_MVA'])\n        })\n\n    # Feasibility check\n    max_voltage_violation = 0\n    for i in range(info['n_bus']):\n        Vm = solution['Vm'][i]\n        Vmin = info['bus_data'][i, 12]\n        Vmax = info['bus_data'][i, 11]\n        violation = max(0, Vmin - Vm, Vm - Vmax)\n        max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, violation)\n\n    max_branch_overload = 0\n    for br in branch_flows:\n        if br['rate_MVA'] > 0:\n            overload = max(0, br['Sij_MVA'] - br['rate_MVA'], br['Sji_MVA'] - br['rate_MVA'])\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n\n    # Try to infer from solution quality\n        solver_status = 'suboptimal'\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['objective']),\n            'total_load_MW': total_load_MW,\n            'total_load_MVAr': total_load_MVAr,\n            'total_generation_MW': total_gen_MW,\n            'total_generation_MVAr': total_gen_MVAr,\n            'total_losses_MW': total_losses_MW,\n            'solver_status': solver_status\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': most_loaded_branches,\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': 0.001,\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': 0.001,\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_voltage_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_branch_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main function\"\"\"\n\n    # {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/ac-branch-pi-model\n\n# AC Branch Pi-Model + Transformer Handling\n\nImplement the exact branch power flow equations in `acopf-math-model.md` using MATPOWER branch data:\n\n```\n[F_BUS, T_BUS, BR_R, BR_X, BR_B, RATE_A, RATE_B, RATE_C, TAP, SHIFT, BR_STATUS, ANGMIN, ANGMAX]\n```\n\n## Quick start\n\n- Use `scripts/branch_flows.py` to compute per-unit branch flows.\n- Treat the results as **power leaving** the “from” bus and **power leaving** the “to” bus (i.e., compute both directions explicitly).\n\nExample:\n\n```python\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom scripts.branch_flows import compute_branch_flows_pu, build_bus_id_to_idx\n\ndata = json.load(open(\"/root/network.json\"))\nbaseMVA = float(data[\"baseMVA\"])\nbuses = np.array(data[\"bus\"], dtype=float)\nbranches = np.array(data[\"branch\"], dtype=float)\n\nbus_id_to_idx = build_bus_id_to_idx(buses)\n\nVm = buses[:, 7]  # initial guess VM\nVa = np.deg2rad(buses[:, 8])  # initial guess VA\n\nbr = branches[0]\nP_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = compute_branch_flows_pu(Vm, Va, br, bus_id_to_idx)\n\nS_ij_MVA = (P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nS_ji_MVA = (P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nprint(S_ij_MVA, S_ji_MVA)\n```\n\n## Model details (match the task formulation)\n\n### Per-unit conventions\n\n- Work in per-unit internally.\n- Convert with `baseMVA`:\n  - \\(P_{pu} = P_{MW} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(Q_{pu} = Q_{MVAr} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(|S|_{MVA} = |S|_{pu} \\cdot baseMVA\\)\n\n### Transformer handling (MATPOWER TAP + SHIFT)\n\n- Use \\(T_{ij} = tap \\cdot e^{j \\cdot shift}\\).\n- **Implementation shortcut (real tap + phase shift):**\n  - If `abs(TAP) < 1e-12`, treat `tap = 1.0` (no transformer).\n  - Convert `SHIFT` from degrees to radians.\n  - Use the angle shift by modifying the angle difference:\n    - \\(\\delta_{ij} = \\theta_i - \\theta_j - shift\\)\n    - \\(\\delta_{ji} = \\theta_j - \\theta_i + shift\\)\n\n### Series admittance\n\nGiven `BR_R = r`, `BR_X = x`:\n\n- If `r == 0 and x == 0`, set `g = 0`, `b = 0` (avoid divide-by-zero).\n- Else:\n  - \\(y = 1/(r + jx) = g + jb\\)\n  - \\(g = r/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n  - \\(b = -x/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n\n### Line charging susceptance\n\n- `BR_B` is the **total** line charging susceptance \\(b_c\\) (per unit).\n- Each end gets \\(b_c/2\\) in the standard pi model.\n\n## Power flow equations (use these exactly)\n\nLet:\n- \\(V_i = |V_i| e^{j\\theta_i}\\), \\(V_j = |V_j| e^{j\\theta_j}\\)\n- `tap` is real, `shift` is radians\n- `inv_t = 1/tap`, `inv_t2 = inv_t^2`\n\nThen the real/reactive power flow from i→j is:\n\n- \\(P_{ij} = g |V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ij} + b\\sin\\delta_{ij})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ij} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ij} - b\\cos\\delta_{ij})\\)\n\nAnd from j→i is:\n\n- \\(P_{ji} = g |V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ji} + b\\sin\\delta_{ji})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ji} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ji} - b\\cos\\delta_{ji})\\)\n\nCompute apparent power:\n\n- \\(|S_{ij}| = \\sqrt{P_{ij}^2 + Q_{ij}^2}\\)\n- \\(|S_{ji}| = \\sqrt{P_{ji}^2 + Q_{ji}^2}\\)\n\n## Common uses\n\n### Enforce MVA limits (rateA)\n\n- `RATE_A` is an MVA limit (may be 0 meaning “no limit”).\n- Enforce in both directions:\n  - \\(|S_{ij}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n  - \\(|S_{ji}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n\n### Compute branch loading %\n\nFor reporting “most loaded branches”:\n\n- `loading_pct = 100 * max(|S_ij|, |S_ji|) / RATE_A` if `RATE_A > 0`, else 0.\n\n### Aggregate bus injections for nodal balance\n\nTo build the branch flow sum for each bus \\(i\\):\n\n- Add \\(P_{ij}, Q_{ij}\\) to bus i\n- Add \\(P_{ji}, Q_{ji}\\) to bus j\n\nThis yields arrays `P_out[i]`, `Q_out[i]` such that the nodal balance can be written as:\n\n- \\(P^g - P^d - G^s|V|^2 = P_{out}\\)\n- \\(Q^g - Q^d + B^s|V|^2 = Q_{out}\\)\n\n## Sanity checks (fast debug)\n\n- With `SHIFT=0` and `TAP=1`, if \\(V_i = V_j\\) and \\(\\theta_i=\\theta_j\\), then \\(P_{ij}\\approx 0\\) and \\(P_{ji}\\approx 0\\) (lossless only if r=0).\n- For a pure transformer (`r=x=0`) you should not get meaningful flows; treat as `g=b=0` (no series element).\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"eb5b3d47-1aee-4716-b32b-a55141fed861","uuid":"1237f793-a2a8-4ddb-81dd-cd844651ddf8","isSynthetic":true}
